import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate, Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth, User } from './contexts/AuthContext';
import { AlertProvider } from './contexts/AlertContext';
import { api } from './services/api';
import Layout from './components/Layout';
import LoginPage from './pages/Login';
import DashboardPage from './pages/Dashboard';
import AssetsPage from './pages/Assets';
import WorkOrdersPage from './pages/WorkOrders';
import FuelPage from './pages/Fuel';
import SuperAdminPage from './pages/SuperAdmin';
import UsersPage from './pages/Users';
import InventoryPage from './pages/Inventory';
import TeamsPage from './pages/Teams';
import MaintenancePlansPage from './pages/MaintenancePlans';
import CompanyDetailsPage from './pages/CompanyDetails';
import ItLogsPage from './pages/ItLogs';
import {
  Building2,
  Truck,
  ClipboardList,
  Fuel,
  TrendingUp,
  Plus,
  Wrench,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Calendar,
  Layers,
  Sparkles,
  RefreshCw,
  Search,
  Filter,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  Loader2,
  UploadCloud,
  Camera
} from 'lucide-react';

// Formatação de Moeda em Meticais Moçambicanos (MZN)
const formatMZN = (val: number) => {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-MZ', {
    style: 'currency',
    currency: 'MZN',
    minimumFractionDigits: 2
  }).format(val).replace('MTn', 'MZN').trim() + ' MZN';
};

// ==========================================
// PÁGINA DE PERFIL DO UTILIZADOR
// ==========================================
const LoadingFallback: React.FC = () => (
  <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900">
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
      <div className="w-8 h-8 border-3 border-blue-200 border-t-blue-600 rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
      <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 font-medium animate-pulse">A verificar sessão...</p>
    </div>
  </div>
);

const ProfilePage: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, loading, setUser } = useAuth();
  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState(user?.avatar_url || '');
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const fileInputRef = React.useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setAvatar(user?.avatar_url || '');
  }, [user?.avatar_url]);

  if (loading) return <LoadingFallback />;
  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;

  const handleAvatarUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setUploading(true);
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('avatar', file);

    try {
      const response = await api.post('/auth/me/avatar', formData, {
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'multipart/form-data' }
      });
      const avatarUrl = response.data?.avatar_url || response.data?.user?.avatar_url;
      if (!avatarUrl) throw new Error('O servidor não devolveu o endereço da fotografia.');
      setAvatar(avatarUrl);
      setUser({ ...user, avatar_url: avatarUrl });
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao atualizar fotografia do perfil:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Não foi possível atualizar a fotografia.');
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
      e.target.value = '';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-3xl">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">Meu Perfil</h1>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-6 space-y-6 shadow-none">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-5 border-b border-slate-100 pb-6">
          {/* Fotografia não clicável diretamente (Prompt 2 - Requisito 1) */}
          <div className="relative w-16 h-16 rounded-md overflow-hidden border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            {avatar ? (
              <img src={avatar} alt="Fotografia de perfil" className="w-16 h-16 rounded-md object-cover" />
            ) : (
              <span className="font-bold text-2xl uppercase">
                {user.fullName ? user.fullName.substring(0, 2) : 'US'}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">{user.fullName || user.email}</h2>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs">{user.email}</p>

            {/* Input escondido e Botão Explícito "Alterar" */}
            <div className="mt-2.5 flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                ref={fileInputRef}
                type="file"
                accept="image/jpeg, image/png, image/webp"
                className="hidden"
                onChange={handleAvatarUpload}
                disabled={uploading}
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                disabled={uploading}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold rounded-md shadow-none flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {uploading ? <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin text-blue-700" /> : <Camera size={13} />}
                <span>Alterar</span>
              </button>
              {uploading && <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">A processar nova foto...</span>}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Informações Pessoais do Utilizador (Sem dados da empresa - Prompt 2 Requisito 2) */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block">Nível de Acesso (Cargo)</span>
            <span className="text-sm text-slate-900 font-semibold block mt-1 capitalize">
              {user.role === 'tenant_admin' ? 'Gestor de Contrato (Tenant Admin)' : user.role === 'super_admin' ? 'Super Administrador' : user.role === 'supervisor' ? 'Supervisor Operacional' : user.role === 'technician' ? 'Técnico de Manutenção' : 'Motorista / Operador'}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block">Idioma & Região</span>
            <span className="text-sm text-slate-900 block mt-1">Português (Moçambique)</span>
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block">Fuso Horário Técnico</span>
            <span className="text-sm text-slate-900 block mt-1">GMT+2 (Moçambique - Maputo)</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ==========================================
// ROTEAMENTO PRINCIPAL E PROTEÇÃO
// ==========================================
const ProtectedRoute: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode; requiresSuperAdmin?: boolean }> = ({ children, requiresSuperAdmin = false }) => {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return <LoadingFallback />;
  }

  if (!user) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  if (requiresSuperAdmin && user.role !== 'super_admin') {
    return <Navigate to="/app/dashboard" replace />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
};

// Componente Guard para redirecionar super_admin ao tentar aceder a /app/dashboard
const DashboardGuard: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) return <LoadingFallback />;

  if (user?.role === 'super_admin') {
    return <Navigate to="/super-admin" replace />;
  }

  return <DashboardPage />;
};

// Componente para gerir o redirecionamento dinâmico na rota raiz /
const RootRedirect: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) return <LoadingFallback />;
  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;

  if (user.role === 'super_admin') {
    return <Navigate to="/super-admin" replace />;
  }

  return <Navigate to="/app/dashboard" replace />;
};

// Componente para proteger estritamente o módulo de Gestão de Utilizadores para super_admin
const SuperAdminUsersGuard: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) return <LoadingFallback />;

  if (user?.role !== 'super_admin') {
    return <Navigate to="/app/dashboard" replace />;
  }

  return <UsersPage />;
};

export default function App() {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <AlertProvider>
        <BrowserRouter>
          <Routes>
            {/* Rota Pública */}
            <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />

            {/* Atalho direto para /users */}
            <Route path="/users" element={<Navigate to="/app/users" replace />} />

            {/* Rota Raiz Dinâmica */}
            <Route path="/" element={<RootRedirect />} />
            
            {/* Rotas de Aplicação Comum (Clientes/Gestores/Mecânicos) */}
            <Route 
              path="/app" 
              element={
                <ProtectedRoute>
                  <Layout />
                </ProtectedRoute>
              }
            >
              <Route index element={<RootRedirect />} />
              <Route path="dashboard" element={<DashboardGuard />} />
              <Route path="assets" element={<AssetsPage />} />
              <Route path="work-orders" element={<WorkOrdersPage />} />
              <Route path="teams" element={<TeamsPage />} />
              <Route path="maintenance-plans" element={<MaintenancePlansPage />} />
              <Route path="fuel" element={<FuelPage />} />
              <Route path="inventory" element={<InventoryPage />} />
              <Route path="users" element={<SuperAdminUsersGuard />} />
              <Route path="company" element={<CompanyDetailsPage />} />
              <Route path="it-logs" element={<ItLogsPage />} />
              <Route path="profile" element={<ProfilePage />} />
              <Route path="settings" element={<Navigate to="/app/dashboard" replace />} />
            </Route>

            {/* Rota Privada Super Admin */}
            <Route 
              path="/super-admin" 
              element={
                <ProtectedRoute requiresSuperAdmin>
                  <Layout />
                </ProtectedRoute>
              }
            >
              <Route index element={<SuperAdminPage />} />
            </Route>

            {/* Rota de Fallback */}
            <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/login" replace />} />
          </Routes>
        </BrowserRouter>
      </AlertProvider>
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/AppAlertModal.tsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  AlertCircle,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Info,
  HelpCircle,
  X
} from 'lucide-react';

export type AlertType = 'error' | 'warning' | 'success' | 'info';

export interface AlertOptions {
  title?: string;
  message: string;
  type?: AlertType;
  confirmText?: string;
}

export interface ConfirmOptions {
  title?: string;
  message: string;
  confirmText?: string;
  cancelText?: string;
  danger?: boolean;
  onConfirm?: () => void;
  onCancel?: () => void;
}

interface AppAlertModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onConfirm: () => void;
  isConfirm: boolean;
  options: AlertOptions | null;
  confirmOptions: ConfirmOptions | null;
}

export const AppAlertModal: React.FC<AppAlertModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onConfirm,
  isConfirm,
  options,
  confirmOptions,
}) => {
  const confirmButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      confirmButtonRef.current?.focus();
    }, 50);

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        onClose();
      } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        const activeTag = document.activeElement?.tagName;
        if (activeTag !== 'TEXTAREA') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (isConfirm) {
            onConfirm();
          } else {
            onClose();
          }
        }
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [isOpen, isConfirm, onClose, onConfirm]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const type = options?.type || (confirmOptions?.danger ? 'warning' : 'info');
  const title = isConfirm
    ? confirmOptions?.title || 'Confirmação Necessária'
    : options?.title || (type === 'error' ? 'Erro' : type === 'success' ? 'Sucesso' : 'Atenção');
  const message = isConfirm ? confirmOptions?.message : options?.message;
  const confirmText = isConfirm
    ? confirmOptions?.confirmText || 'Confirmar'
    : options?.confirmText || 'Entendido';
  const cancelText = confirmOptions?.cancelText || 'Cancelar';

  const typeStyles = {
    error: {
      bg: 'bg-rose-50',
      border: 'border-rose-200',
      text: 'text-rose-600',
      button: 'bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 active:bg-rose-800 text-white',
      icon: <AlertCircle size={24} className="text-rose-600" />,
    },
    warning: {
      bg: 'bg-amber-50',
      border: 'border-amber-200',
      text: 'text-amber-600',
      button: 'bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 active:bg-amber-800 text-white',
      icon: <AlertTriangle size={24} className="text-amber-600" />,
    },
    success: {
      bg: 'bg-emerald-50',
      border: 'border-emerald-200',
      text: 'text-emerald-600',
      button: 'bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 active:bg-emerald-800 text-white',
      icon: <CheckCircle2 size={24} className="text-emerald-600" />,
    },
    info: {
      bg: 'bg-blue-50',
      border: 'border-blue-200',
      text: 'text-blue-600',
      button: 'bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 active:bg-blue-900 text-white',
      icon: isConfirm ? <HelpCircle size={24} className="text-blue-600" /> : <Info size={24} className="text-blue-600" />,
    },
  }[type];

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="alert-dialog-title"
      aria-describedby="alert-dialog-description"
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl shadow-2xl max-w-md w-full p-6 relative overflow-hidden transition-all transform animate-scale-up"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          aria-label="Fechar"
        >
          <X size={18} />
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
          <div
            className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 border ${typeStyles.bg} ${typeStyles.border}`}
          >
            {typeStyles.icon}
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 pt-0.5">
            <h3
              id="alert-dialog-title"
              className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 mb-1 leading-snug"
            >
              {title}
            </h3>
            <p
              id="alert-dialog-description"
              className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap break-words"
            >
              {message}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100">
          {isConfirm && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              {cancelText}
            </button>
          )}

          <button
            ref={confirmButtonRef}
            type="button"
            onClick={isConfirm ? onConfirm : onClose}
            className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer ${typeStyles.button}`}
          >
            {confirmText}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


=== FILE: src/components/BrandLogo.tsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { BRANDING } from '../config/branding';

interface BrandLogoProps {
  variant?: 'light' | 'dark' | 'icon';
  collapsed?: boolean;
  className?: string;
  alt?: string;
}

export const BrandLogo: React.FC<BrandLogoProps> = ({
  variant = 'light',
  collapsed = false,
  className = 'h-8 w-auto',
  alt = 'Avexas CMMS',
}) => {
  const logoSrc = collapsed
    ? BRANDING.logos.icon
    : BRANDING.logos[variant] || BRANDING.logos.light;

  return (
    <img
      src={logoSrc}
      alt={alt}
      draggable={false}
      onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
      onDragStart={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
      className={`object-contain select-none pointer-events-none ${className}`}
      style={{ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none', WebkitTouchCallout: 'none' }}
    />
  );
};

export default BrandLogo;


=== FILE: src/components/DraftBanner.tsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { FileText, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';

export interface DraftBannerProps {
  onDiscard: () => void;
  savedAt?: number | null;
}

export const DraftBanner: React.FC<DraftBannerProps> = ({ onDiscard, savedAt }) => {
  const timeText = savedAt
    ? `salvo em ${new Date(savedAt).toLocaleDateString('pt-MZ')} às ${new Date(savedAt).toLocaleTimeString('pt-MZ', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}`
    : 'salvo anteriormente';

  return (
    <div className="p-3 bg-amber-50/90 border border-amber-200/90 rounded-md flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2.5 text-xs text-amber-950 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
        <FileText size={16} className="text-amber-700 shrink-0" />
        <span className="truncate">
          <strong className="font-semibold">Rascunho recuperado:</strong> Dados restaurados ({timeText}).
        </span>
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onDiscard}
        className="self-end sm:self-auto text-rose-600 hover:text-rose-800 text-[11px] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0 py-0.5 px-2 rounded hover:bg-rose-50"
        title="Descartar rascunho e limpar formulário"
      >
        <Trash2 size={12} />
        <span>Descartar rascunho</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
};

export default DraftBanner;

import React from 'react';
import { FileText, Trash2, Save, X } from 'lucide-react';

export interface DraftConfirmModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  moduleName: string;
  onSaveDraft: () => void;
  onDiscard: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

export const DraftConfirmModal: React.FC<DraftConfirmModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  moduleName,
  onSaveDraft,
  onDiscard,
  onCancel
}) => {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
      <div 
        role="dialog"
        aria-labelledby="draft-confirm-title"
        aria-modal="true"
        className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-xl w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden animate-scale-up"
      >
        <div className="p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <FileText size={20} />
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <h3 id="draft-confirm-title" className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
                Guardar como Rascunho?
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-1 leading-relaxed">
                Você já começou a preencher este registo de <strong className="text-slate-800 font-semibold">{moduleName}</strong>. 
                Deseja guardar as informações para continuar mais tarde ou descartar definitivamente?
              </p>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onCancel}
              className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 p-1 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              aria-label="Voltar ao formulário"
            >
              <X size={16} />
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            {/* Botão de descarte com apenas o ícone de lixeira conforme solicitado */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onDiscard}
              className="p-2 text-rose-600 hover:text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center"
              title="Descartar e sair"
              aria-label="Descartar e sair"
            >
              <Trash2 size={16} />
            </button>

            {/* Ações: Continuar e Sim, perfeitamente visíveis sem corte */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onCancel}
                className="px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-300 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                Continuar
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onSaveDraft}
                className="px-5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 active:bg-blue-900 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-xs whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                Sim
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default DraftConfirmModal;


=== FILE: src/components/DraftsListView.tsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { DraftItem } from '../services/draftService';
import { triggerGlobalConfirm } from '../contexts/AlertContext';
import { FileText, Trash2, Edit2, Clock, Plus, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

interface DraftsListViewProps {
  moduleName: string; // e.g. "Ativo", "Ordem de Serviço", "Plano de Manutenção", "Abastecimento", "Item de Inventário"
  drafts: DraftItem[];
  onResumeDraft: (draft: DraftItem) => void;
  onDeleteDraft: (draftId: string) => void;
  onNewItem?: () => void;
}

export const DraftsListView: React.FC<DraftsListViewProps> = ({
  moduleName,
  drafts,
  onResumeDraft,
  onDeleteDraft,
  onNewItem
}) => {
  if (drafts.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-12 text-center space-y-3">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-slate-50 text-slate-400 border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
          <FileText size={22} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">
            Nenhum rascunho guardado de {moduleName}
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 max-w-md mx-auto mt-1">
            Quando iniciar o preenchimento de um novo {moduleName.toLowerCase()} e sair antes de concluir, poderá guardá-lo como rascunho para continuar mais tarde.
          </p>
        </div>
        {onNewItem && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onNewItem}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-xs mt-2"
          >
            <Plus size={14} />
            <span>Criar Novo {moduleName}</span>
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Banner Informativo */}
      <div className="bg-amber-50/70 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg p-3 text-xs text-amber-900 flex items-start gap-2.5">
        <AlertCircle size={16} className="text-amber-700 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <div className="leading-relaxed">
          <span className="font-bold">Lista de Rascunhos:</span> Estes registos contêm informações parciais guardadas no seu dispositivo. Não foram submetidos à base de dados para não concorrer com os registos ativos. Clique em <strong>Continuar</strong> para concluir o preenchimento e registar oficialmente.
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tabela de Rascunhos */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg overflow-hidden shadow-none">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-xs">
            <thead className="bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 uppercase text-[10px] tracking-wider font-semibold border-b border-slate-200">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-3">Identificação do Rascunho</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3">Módulo</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3">Última Gravação</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Estado</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right">Ações</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {drafts.map((draft) => (
                <tr key={draft.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/70 transition-colors">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                      <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-md bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-700 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                        <FileText size={15} />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-xs truncate max-w-md">
                          {draft.title || 'Rascunho Sem Título'}
                        </div>
                        {draft.subtitle && (
                          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate max-w-md mt-0.5">
                            {draft.subtitle}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-slate-600 font-medium whitespace-nowrap">
                    {moduleName}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-slate-600 whitespace-nowrap">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-[11px]">
                      <Clock size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
                      <span>
                        {new Date(draft.timestamp).toLocaleString('pt-MZ', {
                          day: '2-digit',
                          month: '2-digit',
                          year: 'numeric',
                          hour: '2-digit',
                          minute: '2-digit'
                        })}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-center whitespace-nowrap">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200">
                      Rascunho Pendente
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onResumeDraft(draft)}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 border border-blue-200 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5"
                        title="Continuar a preencher este rascunho"
                      >
                        <Edit2 size={12} />
                        <span>Continuar</span>
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={async () => {
                          const confirmed = await triggerGlobalConfirm({
                            title: 'Descartar Rascunho',
                            message: `Deseja descartar definitivamente este rascunho de "${draft.title}"?`,
                            confirmText: 'Descartar',
                            cancelText: 'Cancelar',
                            danger: true,
                          });
                          if (confirmed) {
                            onDeleteDraft(draft.id);
                          }
                        }}
                        className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                        title="Descartar rascunho"
                        aria-label="Descartar rascunho"
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={14} />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default DraftsListView;


=== FILE: src/components/FleetOperationsReportModal.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import SecondaryView from './SecondaryView';
import { combinationService, CombinationReportItem } from '../services/combinationService';
import { formatMoney } from '../config/countriesCurrencies';
import {
  BarChart3,
  Truck,
  Link as LinkIcon,
  Layers,
  Wrench,
  Fuel,
  FileText,
  Printer,
  Search,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
  XCircle,
  Loader2,
  Clock,
  ShieldCheck,
  Calendar
} from 'lucide-react';

interface FleetOperationsReportModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  activeCombinations: any[];
  assets: any[];
  preselectedCombinationId?: string | null;
}

export const FleetOperationsReportModal: React.FC<FleetOperationsReportModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  activeCombinations,
  assets,
  preselectedCombinationId
}) => {
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'combinations' | 'assets'>('combinations');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [workOrders, setWorkOrders] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [fuelLogs, setFuelLogs] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedCombFilter, setSelectedCombFilter] = useState<string>(preselectedCombinationId || 'all');
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState('all');

  // Carrega dados analíticos de OS e Combustível para agregação
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    let isCurrent = true;
    setLoading(true);

    Promise.allSettled([
      api.get('/work-orders'),
      api.get('/fuel')
    ]).then(([woRes, fuelRes]) => {
      if (!isCurrent) return;

      const rawWo = woRes.status === 'fulfilled'
        ? (Array.isArray(woRes.value.data) ? woRes.value.data : woRes.value.data?.workOrders || woRes.value.data?.data || [])
        : [];
      const rawFuel = fuelRes.status === 'fulfilled'
        ? (Array.isArray(fuelRes.value.data) ? fuelRes.value.data : fuelRes.value.data?.fuelLogs || fuelRes.value.data?.data || [])
        : [];

      setWorkOrders(rawWo);
      setFuelLogs(rawFuel);
      setLoading(false);
    });

    return () => {
      isCurrent = false;
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (preselectedCombinationId) {
      setSelectedCombFilter(preselectedCombinationId);
      setViewMode('combinations');
    }
  }, [preselectedCombinationId]);

  // Gera relatório analítico consolidado
  const combinationReports: CombinationReportItem[] = combinationService.buildCombinationReport(
    activeCombinations,
    assets,
    workOrders,
    fuelLogs
  );

  const filteredCombinations = combinationReports.filter((item) => {
    const matchesFilter = selectedCombFilter === 'all' || item.combinationId === selectedCombFilter || item.combinationCode === selectedCombFilter;
    const matchesSearch = !searchQuery.trim() ||
      item.combinationCode.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      item.truck.codeTag.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      item.truck.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      item.trailers.some(t => t.codeTag.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) || t.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()));
    return matchesFilter && matchesSearch;
  });

  // Agregações globais de conjuntos
  const totalCombCost = filteredCombinations.reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.aggregated.totalCostMzn, 0);
  const totalCombMaintenance = filteredCombinations.reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.aggregated.totalMaintenanceCostMzn, 0);
  const totalCombFuel = filteredCombinations.reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.aggregated.totalFuelCostMzn, 0);
  const operationalCombs = filteredCombinations.filter(c => c.aggregated.overallOperationalStatus === 'operational').length;

  // Filtragem de Ativos Individuais
  const filteredAssets = assets.filter((asset) => {
    const matchesSearch = !searchQuery.trim() ||
      (asset.code_tag || asset.codeTag || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      (asset.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      (asset.license_plate || asset.licensePlate || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());
    const matchesCategory = categoryFilter === 'all' || asset.category === categoryFilter;
    return matchesSearch && matchesCategory;
  });

  // Função de Impressão do Relatório A4 Oficial do Conjunto
  const handlePrintCombinationReport = (comb: CombinationReportItem) => {
    const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
    if (!printWindow) {
      alert('Por favor permita pop-ups para imprimir o relatório do conjunto.');
      return;
    }

    const html = `<!doctype html>
      <html lang="pt">
        <head>
          <meta charset="utf-8" />
          <title>Ficha Operacional de Conjunto - ${comb.combinationCode}</title>
          <style>
            @page { size: A4; margin: 15mm; }
            body { font-family: Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 11px; color: #1e293b; line-height: 1.4; margin: 0; }
            .container { width: 100%; max-width: 190mm; margin: 0 auto; }
            .header { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; border-bottom: 2px solid #0f172a; padding-bottom: 10px; margin-bottom: 14px; }
            .brand { font-size: 18px; font-weight: 800; color: #0f172a; }
            .brand-sub { color: #64748b; font-size: 9px; font-weight: 500; display: block; }
            h1 { font-size: 16px; margin: 0; text-transform: uppercase; }
            .code { font-size: 14px; font-weight: 800; color: #1d4ed8; font-family: monospace; }
            .section-title { background: #f1f5f9; font-weight: bold; padding: 5px 8px; border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; margin-top: 12px; font-size: 10px; text-transform: uppercase; }
            table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 4px; margin-bottom: 8px; }
            th, td { border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; padding: 6px 8px; text-align: left; vertical-align: top; }
            th { background: #f8fafc; font-size: 9px; text-transform: uppercase; }
            .kpi-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr); gap: 8px; margin-top: 8px; }
            .kpi-card { border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; padding: 8px; background: #fafafa; border-radius: 4px; }
            .kpi-label { font-size: 8px; color: #64748b; font-weight: bold; text-transform: uppercase; }
            .kpi-value { font-size: 12px; font-weight: bold; color: #0f172a; margin-top: 2px; }
            .badge { display: inline-block; padding: 2px 6px; font-size: 9px; font-weight: bold; border-radius: 3px; }
            .badge-op { background: #dcfce7; color: #166534; }
            .badge-imp { background: #fef3c7; color: #92400e; }
            .badge-down { background: #fee2e2; color: #991b1b; }
            .signatures { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 40px; margin-top: 40px; }
            .sig-line { border-top: 1px solid #64748b; text-align: center; padding-top: 4px; font-size: 9px; }
          </style>
        </head>
        <body>
          <div class="container">
            <header class="header">
              <div>
                <div class="brand">AVEXAS<span class="brand-sub">CMMS · Gestão de Frota e Conjuntos Rodoviários</span></div>
              </div>
              <div style="text-align: right;">
                <h1>Ficha de Conjunto Operacional</h1>
                <div class="code">${comb.combinationCode}</div>
                <div style="font-size: 9px; color: #64748b;">Emissão: ${new Date().toLocaleDateString('pt-MZ')} às ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString('pt-MZ')}</div>
              </div>
            </header>

            <div class="kpi-grid">
              <div class="kpi-card">
                <div class="kpi-label">Estado Operacional</div>
                <div class="kpi-value">
                  ${comb.aggregated.overallOperationalStatus === 'operational'
                    ? '<span class="badge badge-op">Totalmente Operacional</span>'
                    : comb.aggregated.overallOperationalStatus === 'impaired'
                    ? '<span class="badge badge-imp">Atenção: Trela em Manutenção</span>'
                    : '<span class="badge badge-down">Fora de Serviço (Trator Parado)</span>'}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="kpi-card">
                <div class="kpi-label">Quilometragem (Odómetro)</div>
                <div class="kpi-value">${comb.aggregated.currentMeter.toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} km</div>
              </div>
              <div class="kpi-card">
                <div class="kpi-label">Total de Trelas</div>
                <div class="kpi-value">${comb.aggregated.trailersCount} un</div>
              </div>
              <div class="kpi-card">
                <div class="kpi-label">Custo Consolidado Total</div>
                <div class="kpi-value" style="color: #1d4ed8;">${formatMoney(comb.aggregated.totalCostMzn, 'MZN')}</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="section-title">1. Veículo Trator (Cavalo Mecânico)</div>
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Código / Tag</th>
                  <th>Equipamento</th>
                  <th>Odómetro</th>
                  <th>Estado</th>
                  <th>Custo Manutenção</th>
                  <th>Custo Combustível</th>
                  <th>Total Trator</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                <tr>
                  <td style="font-family: monospace; font-weight: bold; color: #1d4ed8;">${comb.truck.codeTag}</td>
                  <td>${comb.truck.name}</td>

                  <td>${comb.truck.status === 'operational' ? 'Operacional' : 'Em Manutenção'}</td>
                  <td>${formatMoney(comb.truck.maintenanceCostMzn, 'MZN')}</td>
                  <td>${formatMoney(comb.truck.fuelCostMzn, 'MZN')}</td>
                  <td style="font-weight: bold;">${formatMoney(comb.truck.totalCostMzn, 'MZN')}</td>
                </tr>
              </tbody>

            <div class="section-title">2. Trelas e Reboques Associados (Associação Operacional)</div>
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Código / Tag</th>
                  <th>Nome do Ativo</th>
                  <th>Papel da Trela no Conjunto</th>
                  <th>Estado</th>
                  <th>OS Abertas</th>
                  <th>OS Fechadas</th>
                  <th>Custo de Manutenção</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                ${comb.trailers.map(t => `
                  <tr>
                    <td style="font-family: monospace; font-weight: bold;">${t.codeTag}</td>
                    <td>${t.name}</td>
                    <td><strong>${t.roleLabel}</strong></td>
                    <td>${t.status === 'operational' ? '<span class="badge badge-op">Operacional</span>' : '<span class="badge badge-imp">Manutenção</span>'}</td>
                    <td>${t.openWorkOrdersCount}</td>
                    <td>${t.completedWorkOrdersCount}</td>

                  </tr>
                `).join('')}
              </tbody>
            </table>

            <div class="section-title">3. Resumo Económico Consolidado</div>
            <table>
              <tr>

                <td style="font-weight: bold; text-align: right;">${formatMoney(comb.aggregated.totalMaintenanceCostMzn, 'MZN')}</td>
              </tr>
              <tr>
                <td><strong>Custo Total de Combustível Consumido:</strong></td>
                <td style="font-weight: bold; text-align: right;">${formatMoney(comb.aggregated.totalFuelCostMzn, 'MZN')}</td>
              </tr>
              <tr style="background: #f1f5f9;">
                <td><strong>CUSTO TOTAL OPERACIONAL DO CONJUNTO:</strong></td>
                <td style="font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold; color: #1d4ed8; text-align: right;">${formatMoney(comb.aggregated.totalCostMzn, 'MZN')}</td>
              </tr>
            </table>

            <div class="signatures">
              <div class="sig-line">
                <strong>Gestor de Frota / Supervisor Operacional</strong><br />
                Assinatura & Data
              </div>
              <div class="sig-line">
                <strong>Responsável de Manutenção</strong><br />
                Assinatura & Data
              </div>
            </div>

        </body>
      </html>`;

    printWindow.document.open();

    printWindow.document.close();

  return (
    <SecondaryView
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setViewMode('combinations')}
              className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                viewMode === 'combinations'
                  ? 'bg-white text-blue-700 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
              }`}
            >
              <LinkIcon size={14} />
              <span>Por Conjunto Operacional (Camião + Trelas)</span>
            </button>
            <button

              onClick={() => setViewMode('assets')}

                  : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
              }`}
            >

              <Search size={14} className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400" />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder={viewMode === 'combinations' ? 'Buscar conjunto ou matrícula...' : 'Buscar ativo, código ou tag...'}
                value={searchQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-md text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600"
              />
            </div>
            {viewMode === 'combinations' && activeCombinations.length > 0 && (
              <select
                value={selectedCombFilter}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedCombFilter(e.target.value)}
                className="bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-700 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none"
              >
                <option value="all">Todos os Conjuntos ({activeCombinations.length})</option>
                {activeCombinations.map((c) => (
                  <option key={c.id || c.combination_code} value={c.id || c.combination_code}>
                    {c.combination_code || c.combinationCode || 'Conjunto'}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ========================================================
            VISÃO 1: POR CONJUNTO OPERACIONAL (CAMIÃO + TRELAS)
            ======================================================== */}
        {viewMode === 'combinations' && (
          <div className="space-y-6">
            {/* KPI Cards Consolidados */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
              <div className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-xl">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                  Conjuntos Ativos
                </span>
                <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2 mt-1">
                  <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-mono">
                    {filteredCombinations.length}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-emerald-600 font-medium">
                    {operationalCombs} operacionais
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-xl">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                  Custo Total Consolidado
                </span>
                <span className="text-xl font-bold text-blue-700 font-mono block mt-1">
                  {formatMoney(totalCombCost, 'MZN')}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 block mt-0.5">
                  Manutenção: {formatMoney(totalCombMaintenance, 'MZN')}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-xl">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                  Custo Combustível Tratores
                </span>
                <span className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-mono block mt-1">
                  {formatMoney(totalCombFuel, 'MZN')}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 block mt-0.5">
                  Abastecimentos registados
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-xl">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                  Disponibilidade Operacional
                </span>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
                  <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-mono">
                    {filteredCombinations.length > 0
                      ? `${Math.round((operationalCombs / filteredCombinations.length) * 100)}%`
                      : '100%'}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">taxa da frota</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {loading ? (
              <div className="py-16 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
                <Loader2 size={24} className="animate-spin text-blue-600 mx-auto mb-2" />
                A carregar dados analíticos dos conjuntos...
              </div>
            ) : filteredCombinations.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="py-12 text-center border border-dashed border-slate-300 rounded-xl p-8">
                <LinkIcon size={32} className="text-slate-300 mx-auto mb-2" />
                <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">Nenhum conjunto rodoviário encontrado</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 max-w-md mx-auto mt-1">
                  Crie associações de Camiões Tratores com Trelas Líder e Seguidoras na Gestão de Conjuntos para visualizar os relatórios consolidados.
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-6">
                {filteredCombinations.map((comb) => (
                  <div
                    key={comb.combinationId}
                    className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl bg-white shadow-xs overflow-hidden"
                  >
                    {/* Header do Conjunto */}
                    <div className="bg-slate-50/80 px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm">
                          <LinkIcon size={18} />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-base text-blue-800">
                              {comb.combinationCode}
                            </span>
                            {comb.aggregated.overallOperationalStatus === 'operational' ? (
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200">
                                <CheckCircle2 size={11} />
                                Totalmente Operacional
                              </span>
                            ) : comb.aggregated.overallOperationalStatus === 'impaired' ? (
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200">
                                <AlertTriangle size={11} />
                                Parcialmente Afetado (Trela em Manutenção)
                              </span>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 border border-rose-200">

                                Fora de Serviço (Trator Parado)
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5 block">
                            Ativo desde {comb.startedAt ? new Date(comb.startedAt).toLocaleDateString('pt-MZ') : 'Registo recente'} · {comb.aggregated.trailersCount} trela(s) acoplada(s)
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="text-right">
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold block">
                            Custo Consolidado
                          </span>
                          <span className="font-mono font-bold text-sm text-slate-900">
                            {formatMoney(comb.aggregated.totalCostMzn, 'MZN')}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handlePrintCombinationReport(comb)}
                          className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                          title="Imprimir Ficha Completa do Conjunto A4"
                        >
                          <Printer size={14} />
                          <span>Imprimir Ficha (A4)</span>
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Detalhes do Camião Trator e Trelas */}
                    <div className="p-5 space-y-4">
                      
                      {/* Trator */}
                      <div className="bg-slate-50/50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-3.5">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs mb-2">
                          <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                            <Truck size={14} className="text-blue-700" />
                            Veículo Trator (Cavalo Mecânico)
                          </span>
                          <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold ${
                            comb.truck.status === 'operational'
                              ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200'
                              : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200'
                          }`}>
                            {comb.truck.status === 'operational' ? 'Operacional' : 'Em Manutenção'}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-5 gap-3 text-xs">
                          <div>
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase block">Tag / Código</span>
                            <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-slate-900">{comb.truck.codeTag}</span>
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase block">Equipamento</span>
                            <span className="font-medium text-slate-800 truncate block">{comb.truck.name}</span>
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase block">Quilometragem</span>
                            <span className="font-mono text-slate-900">{comb.truck.currentMeter.toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} km</span>
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase block">Custo Manutenção</span>
                            <span className="font-mono text-slate-900">{formatMoney(comb.truck.maintenanceCostMzn, 'MZN')}</span>
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase block">Custo Combustível</span>
                            <span className="font-mono text-slate-900">{formatMoney(comb.truck.fuelCostMzn, 'MZN')}</span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* Trelas Associadas com Papel Operacional */}
                      <div className="space-y-2">
                        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wide block">
                          Trelas e Reboques Acoplados ({comb.trailers.length})
                        </span>
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                          {comb.trailers.map((trailer) => (
                            <div
                              key={trailer.id}
                              className="border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-3.5 bg-white space-y-2"
                            >
                              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-xs text-blue-700">
                                    {trailer.codeTag}
                                  </span>
                                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border border-amber-200">
                                    {trailer.roleLabel}
                                  </span>
                                </div>
                                <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold ${
                                  trailer.status === 'operational'
                                    ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200'
                                    : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200'
                                }`}>
                                  {trailer.status === 'operational' ? 'Operacional' : 'Manutenção'}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                              <span className="text-xs text-slate-800 font-medium block truncate">
                                {trailer.name}
                              </span>
                              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-[11px] pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 text-slate-600">
                                <div>
                                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block uppercase">OS Abertas</span>
                                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{trailer.openWorkOrdersCount}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block uppercase">OS Concluídas</span>
                                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{trailer.completedWorkOrdersCount}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block uppercase">Custo Trela</span>
                                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-blue-700">{formatMoney(trailer.maintenanceCostMzn, 'MZN')}</span>
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>

                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ========================================================
            VISÃO 2: POR ATIVO INDIVIDUAL
            ======================================================== */}
        {viewMode === 'assets' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs text-slate-600">
              <span>A exibir <strong>{filteredAssets.length}</strong> ativos individuais com métricas isoladas</span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">Filtrar Categoria:</span>
                <select
                  value={categoryFilter}
                  onChange={(e) => setCategoryFilter(e.target.value)}
                  className="bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1 text-xs text-slate-800"
                >
                  <option value="all">Todas</option>
                  <option value="CAT-03">CAT-03 - Veículos Pesados (Camiões)</option>
                  <option value="CAT-06">CAT-06 - Reboques e Semirreboques</option>
                  <option value="CAT-01">CAT-01 - Ligeiros</option>
                  <option value="CAT-02">CAT-02 - Comerciais Ligeiros</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-slate-200 rounded-xl">
              <table className="w-full text-left text-xs border-collapse">
                <thead className="bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 uppercase text-[10px]">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="px-3.5 py-3 font-semibold">Código / Tag</th>
                    <th className="px-3.5 py-3 font-semibold">Equipamento</th>
                    <th className="px-3.5 py-3 font-semibold">Associação Operacional</th>
                    <th className="px-3.5 py-3 font-semibold">Quilometragem / Horímetro</th>
                    <th className="px-3.5 py-3 font-semibold text-center">Estado</th>
                    <th className="px-3.5 py-3 font-semibold text-right">Custo Manutenção</th>
                    <th className="px-3.5 py-3 font-semibold text-right">Custo Combustível</th>
                    <th className="px-3.5 py-3 font-semibold text-right">Custo Total</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                  {filteredAssets.map((asset) => {
                    const tag = asset.code_tag || asset.codeTag || 'S/N';
                    const meter = Number(asset.current_meter || asset.currentMeter || 0);
                    
                    // Verifica se o ativo está associado a algum conjunto
                    const activeComb = activeCombinations.find((c) => {
                      const truckId = c.truck_id || c.truckId || c.tractor_id;
                      const leaderId = c.leader_trailer_id || c.leaderTrailerId;
                      const followerId = c.follower_trailer_id || c.followerTrailerId;
                      return truckId === asset.id || leaderId === asset.id || followerId === asset.id;
                    });

                    let roleInComb = 'Ativo Individual';
                    if (activeComb) {
                      const combCode = activeComb.combination_code || activeComb.combinationCode || 'CONJ';
                      const truckId = activeComb.truck_id || activeComb.truckId || activeComb.tractor_id;
                      const leaderId = activeComb.leader_trailer_id || activeComb.leaderTrailerId;
                      const followerId = activeComb.follower_trailer_id || activeComb.followerTrailerId;

                      if (truckId === asset.id) {
                        roleInComb = `Trator no ${combCode}`;
                      } else if (leaderId === asset.id) {
                        roleInComb = `Líder (Leader) no ${combCode}`;
                      } else if (followerId === asset.id) {
                        roleInComb = `Seguidora (Follower) no ${combCode}`;
                      }
                    }

                    // Custos do ativo
                    const assetWOs = workOrders.filter((wo) => (wo.asset_id || wo.assetId) === asset.id);
                    const maintCost = assetWOs.reduce((acc, wo) => acc + Number(wo.total_cost_mzn || wo.totalCostMzn || 0), 0);
                    const assetFuels = fuelLogs.filter((fl) => (fl.asset_id || fl.assetId) === asset.id);
                    const fuelCost = assetFuels.reduce((acc, fl) => acc + Number(fl.total_cost_mzn || fl.totalCostMzn || 0), 0);
                    const totalCost = maintCost + fuelCost;

                    return (
                      <tr key={asset.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/70 transition-colors">
                        <td className="px-3.5 py-3 font-mono font-bold text-blue-700">{tag}</td>
                        <td className="px-3.5 py-3">
                          <span className="font-medium text-slate-900 block">{asset.name}</span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">{asset.brand} {asset.model}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3.5 py-3">
                          {activeComb ? (
                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-blue-50 text-blue-800 border border-blue-200">
                              <LinkIcon size={10} />
                              {roleInComb}
                            </span>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] italic">Desassociado</span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3.5 py-3 font-mono text-slate-700">
                          {meter > 0 ? `${meter.toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} km` : '—'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3.5 py-3 text-center">
                          <span className={`inline-flex px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold ${
                            asset.status === 'operational'
                              ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200'
                              : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200'
                          }`}>
                            {asset.status === 'operational' ? 'Operacional' : 'Manutenção'}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3.5 py-3 text-right font-mono text-slate-800">
                          {formatMoney(maintCost, 'MZN')}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3.5 py-3 text-right font-mono text-slate-800">
                          {fuelCost > 0 ? formatMoney(fuelCost, 'MZN') : '—'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3.5 py-3 text-right font-mono font-bold text-blue-800">
                          {formatMoney(totalCost, 'MZN')}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

      </div>
    </SecondaryView>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { NavLink, Outlet, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { QRScannerModal } from './QRScannerModal';
import { BrandLogo } from './BrandLogo';
import { usePreventRefresh } from '../hooks/usePreventRefresh';
import { useVersionCheck } from '../hooks/useVersionCheck';
import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Building2,
  Truck,
  Wrench,
  ClipboardList,
  Fuel,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  X,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  ChevronDown,
  Calendar,
  Bell,

  Settings,
  Shield,

  Layers,
  AlertTriangle,

  Terminal
} from 'lucide-react';
import { NotificationItem, deduplicateNotifications } from '../services/notificationService';

const formatTimeAgo = (dateStr?: string) => {
  if (!dateStr) return '';
  const date = new Date(dateStr);
  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) return dateStr;
  
  const now = new Date();
  const diffInSeconds = Math.floor((now.getTime() - date.getTime()) / 1000);
  
  if (diffInSeconds < 60) return 'Agora mesmo';
  const diffInMinutes = Math.floor(diffInSeconds / 60);
  if (diffInMinutes < 60) return `Há ${diffInMinutes} min`;
  const diffInHours = Math.floor(diffInMinutes / 60);
  if (diffInHours < 24) return `Há ${diffInHours}h`;
  const diffInDays = Math.floor(diffInHours / 24);
  if (diffInDays < 7) return `Há ${diffInDays}d`;
  
  return date.toLocaleDateString('pt-MZ', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
};

export const Layout: React.FC = () => {
  // Ativa a proteção contra recarregamento acidental de página (F5 e fechar aba)
  usePreventRefresh(false);
  const { hasNewVersion, updateApp } = useVersionCheck();

  const {
    user,
    logout,
    loading,
    currentScope,
    setCurrentScope,
    isTenantAdmin,
    isSuperAdmin,
    isSupervisor,
    isTechnician,
    isDriverOperator,
    isFleet,
    isPlant
  } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  
  // Estado para controlar a visibilidade do modal de confirmação de logout
  const [showLogoutConfirm, setShowLogoutConfirm] = useState(false);
  useEscapeKey(() => setShowLogoutConfirm(false), showLogoutConfirm);

  // Estado para controlar se a sidebar está retraída no desktop
  const [isCollapsed, setIsCollapsed] = useState(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('cmms_sidebar_collapsed');
    return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : false;
  });

  // Estado para o submenu sanfona de Manutenção
  const [isMaintenanceOpen, setIsMaintenanceOpen] = useState(false);

  // Estado para controlar o menu hambúrguer em dispositivos móveis
  const [isMobileOpen, setIsMobileOpen] = useState(false);

  // Estado para controlar a abertura do Scanner QR Code Global
  const [isScannerOpen, setIsScannerOpen] = useState(false);

  // Gestão de Notificações
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<NotificationItem[]>([]);
  const [unreadCount, setUnreadCount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [isNotificationOpen, setIsNotificationOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const notificationRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Verificação de conformidade ao entrar (executa no máximo 1 vez por sessão para não duplicar gatilhos)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (user && (user.role === 'tenant_admin' || user.role === 'supervisor')) {
      const sessionKey = `cmms_compliance_checked_${user.id || user.email}`;
      if (!sessionStorage.getItem(sessionKey)) {
        sessionStorage.setItem(sessionKey, 'true');
        api.get('/notifications/check-compliance').catch(() => {});
      }
    }
  }, [user]);

  // Busca notificações na API (GET /notifications) com deduplicação inteligente por assunto
  const fetchNotifications = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/notifications');
      const data = response.data;
      let list: NotificationItem[] = [];

      if (Array.isArray(data)) {
        list = data;
      } else if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
        const possibleList = data.notifications || data.data || data.items || [];
        if (Array.isArray(possibleList)) {
          list = possibleList;
        }
      }

      // Deduplica eventos que andam em pares no mesmo datetime, preservando repetições legítimas em datas distintas
      const uniqueList = deduplicateNotifications(list).slice(0, 30);
      setNotifications(uniqueList);
      const unread = uniqueList.filter(n => !(n.is_read ?? n.read ?? n.isRead)).length;
      setUnreadCount(unread);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Não foi possível obter as notificações:', err);
    }
  };

  // Notificações estáticas - atualiza apenas ao montar o componente
  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) {
      fetchNotifications();
    }
  }, [user]);

  // Fechar dropdown de notificações ao clicar fora
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (notificationRef.current && !notificationRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setIsNotificationOpen(false);
      }
    };
    if (isNotificationOpen) {
      document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    }
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, [isNotificationOpen]);

  // Marcar uma notificação como lida (PATCH /notifications/:id/read para o item e eventuais duplicatas em par)
  const handleMarkAsRead = async (item: NotificationItem) => {
    const isRead = Boolean(item.is_read ?? item.read ?? item.isRead);
    const itemLink = item.link || item.targetUrl || item.target_url || item.url;

    if (!isRead) {
      const idsToMark = item._duplicateIds && item._duplicateIds.length > 0 ? item._duplicateIds : [item.id];
      try {
        await Promise.allSettled(idsToMark.map(id => api.patch(`/notifications/${id}/read`)));
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn(`Erro ao marcar notificação ${item.id} como lida:`, err);
      } finally {
        setNotifications(prev =>
          prev.map(n => (n.id === item.id ? { ...n, is_read: true, read: true, isRead: true } : n))
        );
        setUnreadCount(prev => Math.max(0, prev - 1));
      }
    }

    if (itemLink && itemLink !== '#' && itemLink.trim() !== '') {
      setIsNotificationOpen(false);
      if (itemLink.startsWith('http')) {
        window.open(itemLink, '_blank');
      } else {
        // Garante que o link começa com barra para não quebrar o router
        navigate(itemLink.startsWith('/') ? itemLink : `/${itemLink}`);
      }
    } else {
      setIsNotificationOpen(false);
    }
  };

  // Marcar todas as notificações como lidas (PATCH /notifications/read-all)
  const handleMarkAllAsRead = async () => {
    try {
      await api.patch('/notifications/read-all');
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Erro ao marcar todas as notificações como lidas:', err);
    } finally {
      setNotifications(prev =>
        prev.map(n => ({ ...n, is_read: true, read: true, isRead: true }))
      );
      setUnreadCount(0);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('cmms_sidebar_collapsed', JSON.stringify(isCollapsed));
  }, [isCollapsed]);

  // Fecha o menu móvel ao mudar de rota
  useEffect(() => {
    setIsMobileOpen(false);
  }, [location.pathname]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
          <div className="relative w-12 h-12">
            <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-full border-4 border-blue-200 rounded-full"></div>
            <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-full border-4 border-blue-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
          </div>
          <p className="text-slate-600 font-medium tracking-wide text-sm">A carregar sistema...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 p-6">
        <div className="max-w-md w-full bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-8 text-center shadow-sm">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-red-50 text-red-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6 border border-red-200">
            <Shield size={32} />
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">Acesso Não Autorizado</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-600 text-sm mb-6 leading-relaxed">
            Por favor, inicie sessão com as suas credenciais para gerir os ativos e as frotas do CMMS.
          </p>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/login')}
            className="w-full bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 active:bg-blue-900 text-white font-medium py-2.5 px-4 rounded-md transition-colors text-sm shadow-none"
          >
            Ir para o Login
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Tratamento do papel (role) do utilizador de forma amigável
  const formatRole = (role: string) => {
    switch (role) {
      case 'super_admin':
        return 'Administrador Geral';
      case 'tenant_admin':
        return 'Gestor do Contrato (Admin)';
      case 'supervisor':
        return 'Supervisor Operacional';
      case 'technician':
        return 'Técnico de Campo';
      case 'driver_operator':
        return 'Motorista / Operador';
      default:
        return 'Colaborador';
    }
  };

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    try {
      await logout();
      navigate('/login', { replace: true });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Erro ao terminar sessão:', error);
    }
  };

  // Registo de Saída ao fechar aba ou navegador (Prompt 1 - Requisito 2)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    const handleBeforeUnload = () => {
      try {
        fetch('/api/auth/record-exit', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          keepalive: true,
          credentials: 'include'
        }).catch(() => {});
      } catch {}
    };

    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', handleBeforeUnload);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('beforeunload', handleBeforeUnload);
    };
  }, [user]);

  // Regra de Perfil do Botão Escanear: Apenas para roles DIFERENTES de tenant_admin e super_admin (Prompt 3 - Requisito 1)
  const canScan = user?.role !== 'tenant_admin' && user?.role !== 'super_admin';

  // Isolamento Operacional: Apenas super_admin ou utilizadores explicitamente mistos/both podem alternar escopo (Prompt 4 - Requisito 1 & 2)
  const canSwitchScope = user?.role === 'super_admin' || (
    user?.role !== 'tenant_admin' && (
      user?.operational_scope === 'both' ||
      user?.operational_scope === 'mixed' ||
      user?.operationalScope === 'both' ||
      user?.operationalScope === 'mixed'
    )
  );

  // Definição dos links de navegação com base na role
  const getNavigationItems = () => {
    if (user?.role === 'super_admin') {
      return [
        {
          name: 'Empresas & Contratos',
          path: '/super-admin',
          icon: Building2,
          description: 'Controlo de contratos e tenants'
        },
        {
          name: 'Gestão de Utilizadores',
          path: '/app/users',
          icon: Users,
          description: 'Gestão de contas e permissões'
        }
      ];
    }

    const items: any[] = [];
    const isPlantView = currentScope === 'plant';

    // Primeiro elemento para todos os utilizadores operacionais
    items.push({
      name: 'Dashboard',
      path: '/app/dashboard',
      icon: LayoutDashboard,
      description: 'Indicadores e estado operacional'
    });

    // Todos os escopos têm acesso aos Ativos
    items.push({
      name: isPlantView ? 'Equipamentos Fabris' : 'Frota',
      path: '/app/assets',
      icon: isPlantView ? Wrench : Truck,
      description: isPlantView ? 'Equipamentos industriais' : 'Registo e frotas de viaturas'
    });

    // Grupo Manutenção (Acordeão com Ordens de Serviço, Equipes e Plano de Manutenção)
    items.push({
      name: 'Manutenção',
      type: 'accordion',
      icon: Wrench,
      subItems: [
        {
          name: 'Planos',
          path: '/app/maintenance-plans',
          icon: Calendar
        },
        {
          name: 'Equipes',
          path: '/app/teams',
          icon: Users
        },
        {
          name: 'Ordens',
          path: '/app/work-orders',
          icon: ClipboardList
        }
      ]
    });

    // Combustível disponível para tenant_admin e supervisor apenas se NÃO for plant
    if ((user.role === 'tenant_admin' || user.role === 'supervisor') && !isPlantView) {
      items.push({
        name: 'Combustível',
        path: '/app/fuel',
        icon: Fuel,
        description: 'Consumos e eficiência de combustível'
      });
    }

    if (user.role === 'tenant_admin' || user.role === 'supervisor') {
      items.push({
        name: 'Inventário',
        path: '/app/inventory',
        icon: Layers,
        description: 'Gestão de peças e consumíveis'
      });
    }

    return items;
  };

  const navigationItems = getNavigationItems();

  // Notificações exibidas 100% sincronizadas com a lista e o contador
  const uniqueNotifications = notifications;

  // Identificação da página atual para exibição de breadcrumbs
  const getActivePageName = () => {
    const activeItem = navigationItems.find(item => location.pathname.startsWith(item.path));
    if (activeItem) return activeItem.name;
    if (location.pathname === '/app/profile') return 'Perfil de Utilizador';
    if (location.pathname === '/app/settings') return 'Configurações do Sistema';
    return 'Painel de Controlo';
  };

  return (
