    <div className="flex h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 overflow-hidden font-sans antialiased selection:bg-blue-100 selection:text-blue-900">
      


      {/* SIDEBAR PARA DESKTOP */}
      <aside 
        className={`hidden md:flex flex-col bg-[#0B132B] border-r border-white/10 transition-all duration-300 relative z-20 ${
          isCollapsed ? 'w-20' : 'w-64'
        }`}
      >
        {/* Header da Sidebar */}
        <div className="h-16 flex items-center justify-between px-4 border-b border-white/10 relative">
          {!isCollapsed ? (
            <>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 overflow-hidden">
                <img
                  src="https://i.postimg.cc/QxqWHtpg/avexas-logo-white.png"
                  alt="Avexas CMMS"
                  draggable={false}
                  onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
                  onDragStart={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
                  className="h-10 sm:h-11 w-auto max-w-[170px] object-contain transition-all duration-200"
                  style={{ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none', WebkitTouchCallout: 'none' }}
                />
              </div>

              {/* Botão de Retração */}
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsCollapsed(true)}
                className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 p-1.5 rounded-md transition-all shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
                aria-label="Recolher menu"
              >
                <ChevronLeft size={16} />
              </button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="w-full flex justify-center py-1">
                <img
                  src="https://i.postimg.cc/GtJrMZ6H/avexasfavico.png"
                  alt="Avexas"
                  draggable={false}
                  onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
                  onDragStart={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
                  className="h-8 w-8 object-contain mx-auto"
                  style={{ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none', WebkitTouchCallout: 'none' }}
                />
              </div>

              {/* Botão Flutuante de Expansão */}
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsCollapsed(false)}
                className="absolute -right-3 top-5 z-30 bg-[#0B132B] border border-white/20 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-full p-1 shadow-md transition-all cursor-pointer"
                aria-label="Expandir menu"
              >
                <ChevronRight size={14} />
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Links de Navegação */}
        <nav className="flex-1 px-2.5 py-4 space-y-1.5 overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden">
          {navigationItems.map((item) => {
            if (item.type === 'accordion') {
              const isMaintenanceActive = location.pathname.startsWith('/app/work-orders') ||
                location.pathname.startsWith('/app/teams') ||
                location.pathname.startsWith('/app/maintenance-plans');

              if (isCollapsed) {
                return (
                  <NavLink
                    key="maintenance-group-collapsed"
                    to="/app/work-orders"
                    className={({ isActive }) =>
                      `flex items-center justify-center w-11 h-11 mx-auto rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors group ${
                        isMaintenanceActive
                          ? 'bg-[#0066FF] text-white shadow-none'
                          : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                      }`
                    }
                    title="Manutenção"
                  >
                    <Wrench size={18} className="shrink-0" />
                  </NavLink>
                );
              }

              return (
                <div key="maintenance-group-expanded" className="space-y-1">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setIsMaintenanceOpen(!isMaintenanceOpen)}
                    className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2.5 rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer group ${
                      isMaintenanceActive
                        ? 'text-white font-semibold'
                        : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <Wrench size={18} className={`shrink-0 ${isMaintenanceActive ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-400 group-hover:text-white'}`} />
                      <span className="truncate">Manutenção</span>
                    </div>
                    {isMaintenanceOpen ? <ChevronDown size={15} className="text-slate-400" /> : <ChevronRight size={15} className="text-slate-400" />}
                  </button>

                  {isMaintenanceOpen && (
                    <div className="pl-9 border-l border-white/10 ml-4 space-y-1 my-1">
                      {item.subItems.map((sub: any) => {
                        const SubIcon = sub.icon;
                        return (
                          <NavLink
                            key={sub.path}
                            to={sub.path}
                            className={({ isActive }) =>
                              `flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                                isActive
                                  ? 'bg-[#0066FF] text-white font-semibold shadow-none'
                                  : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                              }`
                            }
                          >
                            <SubIcon size={15} className="shrink-0" />
                            <span className="truncate">{sub.name}</span>
                          </NavLink>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            }

            const Icon = item.icon;
            return (
              <NavLink
                key={item.path}
                to={item.path}
                className={({ isActive }) =>
                  `flex items-center rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors group ${
                    isCollapsed ? 'justify-center w-11 h-11 mx-auto px-0' : 'gap-3 px-3 py-2.5'
                  } ${
                    isActive
                      ? 'bg-[#0066FF] text-white shadow-none'
                      : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                  }`
                }
                title={item.name}
              >
                <Icon 
                  size={18} 
                  className={`shrink-0 transition-transform group-hover:scale-105 duration-150 ${
                    location.pathname.startsWith(item.path) ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-400 group-hover:text-white'
                  }`} 
                />
                
                {!isCollapsed && (
                  <span className="truncate">{item.name}</span>
                )}
              </NavLink>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        {/* Rodapé da Sidebar */}
        <div className="p-3 border-t border-white/10 bg-[#0B132B]">
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowLogoutConfirm(true)}
            className={`flex items-center justify-center rounded-md text-xs font-medium text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-400 hover:bg-rose-500/10 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
              isCollapsed ? 'justify-center w-11 h-11 mx-auto p-0' : 'w-full gap-2 px-3 py-2.5'
            }`}
            title="Terminar Sessão"
          >
            <LogOut size={16} className="shrink-0" />
            {!isCollapsed && <span className="whitespace-nowrap font-medium">Terminar Sessão</span>}
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* DRAWER PARA DISPOSITIVOS MÓVEIS */}
      <div 
        className={`fixed inset-0 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xs z-40 transition-opacity duration-300 md:hidden ${
          isMobileOpen ? 'opacity-100 pointer-events-auto' : 'opacity-0 pointer-events-none'
        }`}
        onClick={() => setIsMobileOpen(false)}
      />

      <aside 
        className={`fixed top-0 bottom-0 left-0 w-72 bg-[#0B132B] border-r border-white/10 z-50 flex flex-col transition-transform duration-300 transform md:hidden ${
          isMobileOpen ? 'translate-x-0' : '-translate-x-full'
        }`}
      >
        {/* Header do Mobile Drawer */}
        <div className="h-16 flex items-center justify-between px-5 border-b border-white/10">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 overflow-hidden">
            <img
              src="https://i.postimg.cc/QxqWHtpg/avexas-logo-white.png"
              alt="Avexas CMMS"
              draggable={false}
              onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
              onDragStart={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
              className="h-10 w-auto object-contain"
              style={{ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none', WebkitTouchCallout: 'none' }}
            />
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => setIsMobileOpen(false)}
            className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white p-1.5 rounded-md transition-all"
            aria-label="Fechar menu"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Links de Navegação Mobile */}
        <nav className="flex-1 px-4 py-6 space-y-1.5 overflow-y-auto">
          {navigationItems.map((item) => {
            if (item.type === 'accordion') {
              const isMaintenanceActive = location.pathname.startsWith('/app/work-orders') ||
                location.pathname.startsWith('/app/teams') ||
                location.pathname.startsWith('/app/maintenance-plans');

              return (
                <div key="maintenance-group-mobile" className="space-y-1">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setIsMaintenanceOpen(!isMaintenanceOpen)}
                    className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                      isMaintenanceActive ? 'text-white font-semibold' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <Wrench size={18} className="shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">Manutenção</span>
                    </div>
                    {isMaintenanceOpen ? <ChevronDown size={16} /> : <ChevronRight size={16} />}
                  </button>

                  {isMaintenanceOpen && (
                    <div className="pl-9 border-l border-white/10 ml-4 space-y-1 my-1">
                      {item.subItems.map((sub: any) => {
                        const SubIcon = sub.icon;
                        return (
                          <NavLink
                            key={sub.path}
                            to={sub.path}
                            onClick={() => setIsMobileOpen(false)}
                            className={({ isActive }) =>
                              `flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                                isActive
                                  ? 'bg-[#0066FF] text-white font-semibold'
                                  : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                              }`
                            }
                          >
                            <SubIcon size={16} className="shrink-0" />
                            <span className="truncate">{sub.name}</span>
                          </NavLink>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            }

            const Icon = item.icon;
            return (
              <NavLink
                key={item.path}
                to={item.path}
                onClick={() => setIsMobileOpen(false)}
                className={({ isActive }) =>
                  `flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                    isActive
                      ? 'bg-[#0066FF] text-white rounded-md text-sm font-medium shadow-none'
                      : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5 rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors'
                  }`
                }
              >
                <Icon size={18} className="shrink-0" />
                <span className="truncate">{item.name}</span>
              </NavLink>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        {/* Rodapé Mobile */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-white/10 bg-[#0B132B]">
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowLogoutConfirm(true)}
            className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-400 hover:bg-rose-500/10 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <LogOut size={16} />
            <span>Terminar Sessão</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* ÁREA DE CONTEÚDO PRINCIPAL (SLATE-50) */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden bg-slate-50">
        
        {/* Banner de Nova Versão */}
        {hasNewVersion && (
          <div className="bg-[#0066FF] text-white px-4 py-2 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium z-50 shadow-md shrink-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
              <span>Uma nova versão do Avexas CMMS está disponível com melhorias e correções.</span>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={updateApp}
              className="bg-white text-[#0066FF] hover:bg-slate-100 font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer text-xs"
            >
              Atualizar Agora
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* TOP BAR / CABEÇALHO */}
        <header className="h-16 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white flex items-center justify-between px-4 md:px-6 shrink-0 z-10">
          
          {/* Lado esquerdo: hambúrguer mobile */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsMobileOpen(true)}
              className="md:hidden text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 p-2 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors"
              aria-label="Abrir menu"
            >
              <Menu size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Lado direito: Notificações, Perfil, Info de Localização Moçambique */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            
            {/* Alternador de Escopo de Negócio (Isolamento rígido - Prompt 4 Requisito 1 & 2) */}
            {canSwitchScope && (
              <div className="flex bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-md border border-slate-200">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setCurrentScope('fleet')}
                  className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold uppercase transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                    currentScope === 'fleet'
                      ? 'bg-blue-700 text-white shadow-none'
                      : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                  }`}
                >
                  Frota
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setCurrentScope('plant')}
                  className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold uppercase transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                    currentScope === 'plant'
                      ? 'bg-blue-700 text-white shadow-none'
                      : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                  }`}
                >
                  Fábrica
                </button>
              </div>
            )}



            {/* Botão de Scan QR Code Global (Header) - Apenas ecrã pequeno (smartphones e tablets) e papéis operacionais autorizados (Prompt 3 Requisito 1) */}
            {canScan && (
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsScannerOpen(true)}
                className="lg:hidden p-2 text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 rounded-md transition-all border border-blue-200 cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold"
                title="Escanear Equipamento (Código QR)"
              >
                <QrCode size={16} />
                <span>Escanear</span>
              </button>
            )}

            {/* Ícone de Notificações com Dropdown Popover */}
            <div className="relative" ref={notificationRef}>
              <button 
                onClick={() => {
                  setIsNotificationOpen(prev => {
                    const next = !prev;
                    if (next) fetchNotifications();
                    return next;
                  });
                }}
                className="p-2 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-all relative border border-transparent hover:border-slate-200 cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center"
                aria-label="Visualizar notificações"
              >
                <Bell size={18} />
                {unreadCount > 0 && (
                  <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 bg-rose-600 text-white text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center min-w-[18px] h-[18px] leading-none shadow-xs">
                    {unreadCount > 99 ? '99+' : unreadCount}
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>

              {/* Popover Dropdown (Design Material Flat Corporativo) */}
              {isNotificationOpen && (
                <div className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 w-80 sm:w-96 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-lg z-50 overflow-hidden">
                  {/* Cabeçalho do Popover */}
                  <div className="px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50/50">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Notificações</span>
                      {unreadCount > 0 && (
                        <span className="bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-blue-200">
                          {unreadCount} {unreadCount === 1 ? 'nova' : 'novas'}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    {unreadCount > 0 && (
                      <button
                        onClick={handleMarkAllAsRead}
                        className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        Marcar todas como lidas
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Lista de Notificações */}
                  <div className="max-h-96 overflow-y-auto divide-y divide-slate-100">
                    {uniqueNotifications.length === 0 ? (
                      <div className="py-8 px-4 text-center flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
                        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-center mb-2 border border-slate-200">
                          <Bell size={18} />
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">Sem notificações pendentes.</p>
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      uniqueNotifications.map((item) => {
                        const isRead = Boolean(item.is_read ?? item.read ?? item.isRead);
                        const title = item.title || item.titulo || 'Notificação';
                        const message = item.message || item.mensagem || '';
                        const createdAt = item.created_at || item.createdAt;

                        return (
                          <div
                            key={item.id}
                            onClick={() => handleMarkAsRead(item)}
                            className={`p-3.5 transition-colors flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer ${
                              isRead ? 'bg-white hover:bg-slate-50' : 'bg-blue-50/40 hover:bg-blue-50/70'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {!isRead ? (
                              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-blue-600 shrink-0 mt-1.5" />
                            ) : (
                              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-transparent shrink-0 mt-1.5" />
                            )}
                            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                              <h4 className={`text-xs ${isRead ? 'font-medium text-slate-800' : 'font-semibold text-slate-900'}`}>
                                {title}
                              </h4>
                              {message && (
                                <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 line-clamp-2 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">
                                  {message}
                                </p>
                              )}
                              {createdAt && (
                                <span className="block text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1">
                                  {formatTimeAgo(createdAt)}
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        );
                      })
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Rodapé do Popover */}
                  <div className="px-4 py-2 border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50 text-center text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-medium">
                    Exibindo as notificações mais recentes
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Separador vertical */}
            <span className="w-px h-6 bg-slate-200 hidden md:inline"></span>

            {/* Perfil com Avatar e Nome */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="hidden sm:flex flex-col items-end">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">{user.fullName || 'Utilizador'}</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-500 uppercase">{formatRole(user.role)}</span>
              </div>
              <NavLink
                to="/app/profile"
                className={({ isActive }) => 
                  `flex items-center justify-center w-9 h-9 rounded-full border-2 transition-all ${
                    isActive 
                      ? 'border-blue-600 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700'
                      : 'border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:border-slate-300'
                  }`
                }
                title="Meu Perfil"
              >
                {user?.avatar_url ? (
                  <img src={user.avatar_url} alt="Avatar" className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full object-cover border-2 border-slate-200" />
                ) : (
                  <span className="relative flex items-center justify-center w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 font-bold text-xs uppercase">
                    {user.fullName ? user.fullName.substring(0, 2) : <User size={16} />}
                    <span
                      title="Clique para adicionar foto"
                      className="absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5 w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white border-2 border-white flex items-center justify-center"
                    >
                      <Camera size={9} />
                    </span>
                  </span>
                )}
              </NavLink>
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* ÁREA DE OUTLET COM ROLAGEM INDEPENDENTE */}
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto bg-slate-50 relative">
          <div className="min-h-full p-4 md:p-8 max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full pb-20 md:pb-8">
            <Outlet />
          </div>
        </main>

        {/* BARRA DE NAVEGAÇÃO INFERIOR MÓVEL */}
        {user.role !== 'super_admin' && (
          <nav className="md:hidden fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-16 bg-white border-t border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-around px-2 z-30 shadow-md">
            <NavLink
              to="/app/assets"
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 h-full text-[10px] font-medium transition-colors ${
                  isActive ? 'text-blue-700 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
                }`
              }
            >
              <Truck size={18} />
              <span className="mt-1">Ativos</span>
            </NavLink>
            
            <NavLink
              to="/app/work-orders"
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 h-full text-[10px] font-medium transition-colors ${
                  isActive ? 'text-blue-700 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
                }`
              }
            >
              <ClipboardList size={18} />
              <span className="mt-1">Ordens</span>
            </NavLink>

            {/* Botão Central de Scan Flutuante */}
            {canScan && (
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsScannerOpen(true)}
                className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center -mt-6 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white w-14 h-14 rounded-full shadow-md active:scale-95 transition-all border-4 border-slate-50 shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
                aria-label="Escanear QR Code de Campo"
              >
                <QrCode size={22} />
              </button>
            )}

            {/* Botão de Combustível condicional */}
            {(user.role === 'tenant_admin' || user.role === 'supervisor') && (
              <NavLink
                to="/app/fuel"
                className={({ isActive }) =>
                  `flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 h-full text-[10px] font-medium transition-colors ${
                    isActive ? 'text-blue-700 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
                  }`
                }
              >
                <Fuel size={18} />
                <span className="mt-1">Combustível</span>
              </NavLink>
            )}

            <NavLink
              to="/app/profile"
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 h-full text-[10px] font-medium transition-colors ${
                  isActive ? 'text-blue-700 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
                }`
              }
            >
              <User size={18} />
              <span className="mt-1">Perfil</span>
            </NavLink>
          </nav>
        )}

        {/* MODAL DE LEITOR QR CODE GLOBAL */}
        <QRScannerModal isOpen={isScannerOpen} onClose={() => setIsScannerOpen(false)} />

        {/* MODAL DE CONFIRMAÇÃO DE LOGOUT */}
        {showLogoutConfirm && (
          <div 
            className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/40 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-xs"
            onClick={() => setShowLogoutConfirm(false)}
          >
            <div 
              className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg max-w-sm w-full p-5 shadow-lg relative"
              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
              {/* Cabeçalho */}
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-3 border-b border-slate-100">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-900 font-bold text-sm">
                  <AlertTriangle size={18} className="text-amber-500 shrink-0" />
                  <span>Terminar Sessão</span>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowLogoutConfirm(false)}
                  className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 p-1 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  aria-label="Fechar"
                >
                  <X size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Texto Descritivo */}
              <p className="text-slate-600 text-xs mb-5 leading-relaxed">
                Tem a certeza de que pretende encerrar a sua sessão de trabalho no CMMS?
              </p>

              {/* Botões */}
              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowLogoutConfirm(false)}
                  className="bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md text-xs px-3 py-1.5 hover:bg-slate-50 font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleLogout}
                  className="bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white rounded-md text-xs px-3 py-1.5 font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Sim, Terminar
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Layout;


=== FILE: src/components/MaintenanceNotificationModal.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useCurrentDomain } from '../config/domainRegistry';
import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';
import { BrandLogo } from './BrandLogo';
import {
  Wrench,
  X,
  Lock,
  UploadCloud,
  Info,
  FileText,
  Trash2,
  Loader2,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
  Image as ImageIcon
} from 'lucide-react';

export interface MaintenanceNotificationModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess?: (createdNotification?: any) => void;
  initialData?: any;
}

interface AttachedFile {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  size: string;
  type: string;
  previewUrl?: string;
  file?: File;
}

export const MaintenanceNotificationModal: React.FC<MaintenanceNotificationModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSuccess,
  initialData
}) => {
  useEscapeKey(onClose, isOpen);
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const domainConfig = useCurrentDomain();
  const isPlant = domainConfig.domain === 'plant';

  const [assets, setAssets] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loadingAssets, setLoadingAssets] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [submitSuccess, setSubmitSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Campos do formulário
  const [requestNumber, setRequestNumber] = useState<string>('Será gerado automaticamente');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'draft' | 'pending_triage' | string>('pending_triage');
  const [selectedAssetId, setSelectedAssetId] = useState<string>('');
  const [selectedAsset, setSelectedAsset] = useState<any | null>(null);

  // Campos de Fábrica e Frota
  const [areaProcess, setAreaProcess] = useState('');
  const [location, setLocation] = useState('');
  const [occurredAt, setOccurredAt] = useState(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    return new Date(now.getTime() - now.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000).toISOString().slice(0, 16);
  });
  const [requesterName, setRequesterName] = useState(user?.fullName || '');
  const [operatorName, setOperatorName] = useState('');
  const [priority, setPriority] = useState<'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'urgent'>('medium');
  const [maintenanceType, setMaintenanceType] = useState<string>('corrective');
  const [meterValue, setMeterValue] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [operationalStatus, setOperationalStatus] = useState<string>('stopped');
  const [problemDescription, setProblemDescription] = useState('');

  // Anexos
  const [attachedFiles, setAttachedFiles] = useState<AttachedFile[]>([]);
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);

  // Carrega lista de ativos
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const fetchAssets = async () => {
      setLoadingAssets(true);
      try {
        const response = await api.get('/assets');
        const list = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : (response.data?.assets || response.data?.data || []);
        
        // Filtrar por domínio
        const domainList = list.filter((asset: any) => {
          const isPlantType = asset.category === 'generator' || asset.category === 'machinery' || asset.type === 'plant' || asset.category_type === 'plant';
          const isFleetType = asset.category === 'vehicle_light' || asset.category === 'vehicle_heavy' || asset.type === 'fleet' || asset.category_type === 'fleet';
          if (isPlant) return !isFleetType;
          return !isPlantType;
        });

        const finalList = domainList.length > 0 ? domainList : list;
        setAssets(finalList);

        if (initialData) {
          const matched = finalList.find((a: any) => a.id === initialData.asset_id || a.id === initialData.assetId);
          if (matched) {
            setSelectedAssetId(matched.id);
            setSelectedAsset(matched);
          }
        } else if (finalList.length > 0 && !selectedAssetId) {
          setSelectedAssetId(finalList[0].id);
          setSelectedAsset(finalList[0]);
          const currentM = finalList[0].current_meter ?? finalList[0].currentMeter ?? '';
          setMeterValue(currentM);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Erro ao carregar ativos para notificação:', err);
      } finally {
        setLoadingAssets(false);
      }
    };

    fetchAssets();

    if (user?.fullName) {
      setRequesterName(user.fullName);
    }
  }, [isOpen, isPlant, user]);

  // Preenche dados ao selecionar um ativo
  const handleAssetSelect = (assetId: string) => {
    setSelectedAssetId(assetId);
    const asset = assets.find(a => a.id === assetId);
    setSelectedAsset(asset || null);
    if (asset) {
      const meter = asset.current_meter ?? asset.currentMeter ?? '';
      setMeterValue(meter);
    }
  };

  // Trata upload de ficheiros
  const handleFileAdd = (files: FileList | null) => {
    if (!files) return;
    const newFiles: AttachedFile[] = [];

    Array.from(files).forEach((file) => {
      const sizeMb = (file.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2);
      const isImg = file.type.startsWith('image/');
      const previewUrl = isImg ? URL.createObjectURL(file) : undefined;

      newFiles.push({
        id: `file-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`,
        name: file.name,
        size: `${sizeMb} MB`,
        type: file.type,
        previewUrl,
        file
      });
    });

    setAttachedFiles(prev => [...prev, ...newFiles]);
  };

  const handleRemoveFile = (fileId: string) => {
    setAttachedFiles(prev => prev.filter(f => f.id !== fileId));
  };

  // Submissão da Notificação (Enviar ou Guardar Rascunho)
  const handleSubmit = async (targetStatus: 'draft' | 'pending_triage') => {
    if (targetStatus === 'pending_triage') {
      if (!selectedAssetId) {
        alert('Por favor, selecione o equipamento ou veículo.');
        return;
      }
      if (!problemDescription.trim()) {
        alert('Por favor, descreva detalhadamente o problema.');
        return;
      }
      if (!requesterName.trim()) {
        alert('Por favor, informe o nome do solicitante.');
        return;
      }
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    setError(null);

    const codeTag = selectedAsset?.code_tag || selectedAsset?.codeTag || '';
    const licensePlate = selectedAsset?.license_plate || selectedAsset?.licensePlate || '';

    // Converter fotos anexadas para base64 para envio persistente
    const base64Files = await Promise.all(
      attachedFiles.map(f => {
        if (!f.file) return Promise.resolve(null);
        return new Promise<string>((resolve) => {
          const reader = new FileReader();
          reader.onloadend = () => resolve(reader.result as string);
          reader.readAsDataURL(f.file!);
        });
      })
    );

    const attachmentsList = base64Files.filter(Boolean);

    const payload = {
      notification_number: initialData?.notification_number || `NOTIF-${Date.now().toString().substring(7)}`,
      asset_id: selectedAssetId,
      assetId: selectedAssetId,
      asset_code_tag: codeTag,
      asset_name: selectedAsset?.name || 'Equipamento',
      license_plate: licensePlate,
      domain: isPlant ? 'plant' : 'fleet',
      area_process: areaProcess,
      areaProcess,
      location: location || 'Não especificado',
      occurred_at: occurredAt || new Date().toISOString(),
      occurredAt,
      requester_name: requesterName,
      requesterName,
      operator_name: operatorName,
      operatorName,
      priority,
      maintenance_type: maintenanceType,
      maintenanceType,
      meter_value: meterValue !== '' ? Number(meterValue) : undefined,
      meterValue: meterValue !== '' ? Number(meterValue) : undefined,
      operational_status: operationalStatus,
      operationalStatus,
      description: problemDescription,
      status: targetStatus,
      attachments: attachmentsList
    };

    try {
      let createdData = null;
      try {
        const res = await api.post('/notifications/maintenance', payload);
        createdData = res.data?.notification || res.data?.data || res.data;
      } catch {
        // Fallback para rota geral
        const res = await api.post('/maintenance-notifications', payload).catch(() => null);
        createdData = res?.data || payload;
      }

      setSubmitSuccess(
        targetStatus === 'draft' 
          ? 'Notificação guardada como rascunho!' 
          : 'Notificação de Manutenção enviada para triagem com sucesso!'
      );

      if (onSuccess) {
        onSuccess(createdData || payload);
      }

      setTimeout(() => {
        setSubmitSuccess(null);
        onClose();
      }, 1500);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao submeter notificação de manutenção:', err);
      setError(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Erro ao submeter notificação.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-4xl rounded-xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden animate-slide-up my-6 flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        
        {/* HEADER DO MODAL */}
        <div className="p-5 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white flex items-center justify-between shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="border-r border-slate-200 pr-4 hidden sm:block">
              <BrandLogo variant="dark" className="h-7 w-auto" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Wrench size={18} className="text-[#0066FF]" />
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Notificação de Manutenção</h2>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                {isPlant
                  ? 'Reporte uma avaria, anomalia ou necessidade de intervenção na fábrica'
                  : 'Reporte uma avaria, anomalia ou necessidade de intervenção na frota'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            aria-label="Fechar modal"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* CORPO DO FORMULÁRIO (SCROLL) */}
        <div className="p-6 space-y-6 flex-1 overflow-y-auto bg-slate-50/50">
          
          {/* TOAST DE SUCESSO OU ERRO */}
          {submitSuccess && (
            <div className="p-4 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-xs rounded-lg flex items-center gap-3 animate-fade-in">
              <CheckCircle2 size={18} className="text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
              <span className="font-semibold">{submitSuccess}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {error && (
            <div className="p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded-lg flex items-center gap-3 animate-fade-in">
              <AlertTriangle size={18} className="text-red-600 shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* BLOCO SUPERIOR DE METADADOS */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 shadow-none">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">
                Nº da Solicitação
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  disabled
                  value={requestNumber}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 text-slate-500 rounded-lg pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs font-mono disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                />
                <Lock size={14} className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400" />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">
                Estado Atual
              </label>
              <div className="flex items-center h-9">
                <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold ${
                  status === 'draft'
                    ? 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200'
                    : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200'
                }`}>
                  {status === 'draft' ? 'Rascunho' : 'Pendente de Triagem'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* BLOCO PRINCIPAL (CARD COM FAIXA SUPERIOR AZUL ESCURO #0B132B) */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-lg border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden shadow-none">
            
            {/* Faixa Superior Azul Escuro */}
            <div className="bg-[#0B132B] px-4 py-2.5 text-white font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center justify-between">
              <span>{isPlant ? 'DADOS DO EQUIPAMENTO / PROCESSO' : 'DADOS DO VEÍCULO / EQUIPAMENTO'}</span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-300 font-mono">
                {isPlant ? 'ÂMBITO FABRIL' : 'FROTA'}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5 space-y-4">
              
              {/* CAMPOS DE FÁBRICA vs FROTA */}
              {isPlant ? (
                /* CAMPOS PARA FÁBRICA (PLANT) */
                <>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Ativo / Equipamento *
                      </label>
                      <select
                        value={selectedAssetId}
                        onChange={(e) => handleAssetSelect(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer outline-none"
                      >
                        {loadingAssets ? (
                          <option value="">A carregar equipamentos...</option>
                        ) : assets.length === 0 ? (
                          <option value="">Nenhum equipamento cadastrado</option>
                        ) : (
                          assets.map((asset) => (
                            <option key={asset.id} value={asset.id}>
                              [{asset.code_tag || asset.codeTag}] {asset.name}
                            </option>
                          ))
                        )}
                      </select>
                      {selectedAsset && (
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1 font-mono">
                          Marca: {selectedAsset.brand || 'N/A'} | Modelo: {selectedAsset.model || 'N/A'} | N.º Série: {selectedAsset.serial_number || selectedAsset.serialNumber || 'N/A'}
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Código do Equipamento
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        readOnly
                        value={selectedAsset?.code_tag || selectedAsset?.codeTag || ''}
                        placeholder="TAG-EQP"
                        className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-mono rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Área / Processo
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={areaProcess}
                        onChange={(e) => setAreaProcess(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: Envase — Linha 02 ou Moagem"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Local da Ocorrência
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={location}
                        onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: Setor de Envase — Linha 02"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </>
              ) : (
                /* CAMPOS PARA FROTA (FLEET) */
                <>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Veículo / Equipamento *
                      </label>
                      <select
                        value={selectedAssetId}
                        onChange={(e) => handleAssetSelect(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer outline-none"
                      >
                        {loadingAssets ? (
                          <option value="">A carregar veículos...</option>
                        ) : assets.length === 0 ? (
                          <option value="">Nenhum veículo cadastrado</option>
                        ) : (
                          assets.map((asset) => (
                            <option key={asset.id} value={asset.id}>
                              [{asset.license_plate || asset.licensePlate || asset.code_tag || asset.codeTag}] {asset.name}
                            </option>
                          ))
                        )}
                      </select>
                      {selectedAsset && (
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1 font-mono">
                          Modelo: {selectedAsset.model || 'N/A'} | Combustível: {selectedAsset.fuel_type || selectedAsset.fuelType || 'Diesel'} | Ano: {selectedAsset.manufacture_year || selectedAsset.manufactureYear || 'N/A'}
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Matrícula / Registro
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        readOnly
                        value={selectedAsset?.license_plate || selectedAsset?.licensePlate || ''}
                        placeholder="MM-00-XX"
                        className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-mono rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Local da Ocorrência
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={location}
                        onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: Frente de lavra — Área Norte ou Estrada EN6"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Motorista / Operador
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={operatorName}
                        onChange={(e) => setOperatorName(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: João Macamo"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}

              {/* CAMPOS COMUNS */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Data e Hora da Ocorrência *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="datetime-local"
                    value={occurredAt}
                    onChange={(e) => setOccurredAt(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 outline-none font-mono"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Nome do Solicitante *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={requesterName}
                    onChange={(e) => setRequesterName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Seu nome completo"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 outline-none"
                  />
                </div>

                {isPlant && (
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Operador / Responsável
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={operatorName}
                      onChange={(e) => setOperatorName(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="ex: Carlos Tembe"
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                )}

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    {isPlant ? 'Horímetro Atual (Horas)' : 'Odómetro Atual (Km)'}
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    value={meterValue}
                    onChange={(e) => setMeterValue(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                    placeholder="ex: 12500"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Prioridade / Nível de Urgência
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={priority}
                    onChange={(e) => setPriority(e.target.value as any)}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer outline-none"
                  >
                    <option value="low">Baixa</option>
                    <option value="medium">Média</option>
                    <option value="high">Alta</option>
                    <option value="urgent">Urgente (Imediato)</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Tipo de Manutenção
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={maintenanceType}
                    onChange={(e) => setMaintenanceType(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer outline-none"
                  >
                    <option value="corrective">Corretiva (Avaria/Paragem)</option>
                    <option value="preventive">Preventiva</option>
                    <option value="predictive">Preditiva</option>
                    <option value="improvement">Melhoria</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Estado Operacional do Ativo
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={operationalStatus}
                    onChange={(e) => setOperationalStatus(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer outline-none"
                  >
                    <option value="stopped">Parado — {isPlant ? 'linha indisponível' : 'indisponível'}</option>
                    <option value="degraded">Operacional — {isPlant ? 'em risco' : 'com restrições'}</option>
                    <option value="normal">Operacional normal</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Descrição Detalhada do Problema / Avaria *
                </label>
                <textarea
                  required
                  rows={4}
                  value={problemDescription}
                  onChange={(e) => setProblemDescription(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Descreva detalhadamente o sintoma, ruído, fuga de óleo, vibração ou falha observada no equipamento..."
                  className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-slate-900 outline-none leading-relaxed"
                />
              </div>

            </div>
          </div>

          {/* BLOCO INFERIOR: ANEXOS E EVIDÊNCIAS */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-lg border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden shadow-none">
            
            {/* Faixa Superior Azul Escuro */}
            <div className="bg-[#0B132B] px-4 py-2.5 text-white font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">
              ANEXOS E EVIDÊNCIAS (FOTOS E DOCUMENTOS)
            </div>

            <div className="p-5 space-y-4">
              
              {/* DROPZONE PONTILHADA */}
              <div
                onDragOver={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setIsDragging(true); }}
                onDragLeave={() => setIsDragging(false)}
                onDrop={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  setIsDragging(false);
                  handleFileAdd(e.dataTransfer.files);
                }}
                className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-xl p-6 text-center transition-colors cursor-pointer relative ${
                  isDragging ? 'border-[#0066FF] bg-blue-50/50' : 'border-slate-300 hover:border-slate-400 bg-slate-50/50'
                }`}
              >
                <input
                  type="file"
                  multiple
                  accept="image/*,video/*,application/pdf"
                  onChange={(e) => handleFileAdd(e.target.files)}
                  className="absolute inset-0 opacity-0 cursor-pointer w-full h-full"
                />
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-blue-50 text-[#0066FF] flex items-center justify-center border border-blue-100">
                    <UploadCloud size={20} />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800">
                      Arraste fotos, vídeos ou documentos aqui
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                      ou clique para selecionar ficheiros do seu dispositivo
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* LISTA DE FICHEIROS ANEXADOS */}
              {attachedFiles.length > 0 && (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wide block">
                    Ficheiros Anexados ({attachedFiles.length})
                  </span>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                    {attachedFiles.map((item) => (
                      <div
                        key={item.id}
                        className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-2.5 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-between text-xs"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 truncate mr-2">
                          {item.previewUrl ? (
                            <img
                              src={item.previewUrl}
                              alt="Preview"
                              className="w-8 h-8 rounded object-cover border border-slate-200 shrink-0"
                            />
                          ) : (
                            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 font-bold text-[10px]">
                              DOC
                            </div>
                          )}
                          <div className="truncate">
                            <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800 block truncate">{item.name}</span>
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block">{item.size}</span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleRemoveFile(item.id)}
                          className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
                          title="Remover ficheiro"
                        >
                          <X size={14} />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* FAIXA INFORMATIVA */}
              <div className="p-3 bg-blue-50/60 border border-blue-200 text-blue-900 text-xs rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <Info size={16} className="text-[#0066FF] shrink-0" />
                <span>
                  Após a triagem, esta solicitação poderá originar uma Ordem de Serviço na equipa de manutenção.
                </span>
              </div>

            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* RODAPÉ DE AÇÕES */}
        <div className="p-5 border-t border-slate-200 bg-white flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3 shrink-0">
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">
            Os campos marcados com * são de preenchimento obrigatório.
          </span>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 w-full sm:w-auto justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              disabled={submitting}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-xs rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              Cancelar
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleSubmit('draft')}
              disabled={submitting}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-800 font-semibold text-xs rounded-lg border border-slate-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              Guardar como rascunho
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleSubmit('pending_triage')}
              disabled={submitting}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#0066FF] hover:bg-blue-700 active:bg-blue-800 text-white font-semibold text-xs rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 shadow-none"
            >
              {submitting ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" />
                  <span>A submeter...</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <span>Enviar solicitação</span>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default MaintenanceNotificationModal;


=== FILE: src/components/QRScannerModal.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Html5Qrcode } from 'html5-qrcode';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';
import {
  X,
  QrCode,
  Loader2,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  FileText,
  File,
  Eye,
  Wrench,
  Gauge,
  Activity,
  ArrowRight,
  UploadCloud,
  ChevronRight,
  Shield,
  HelpCircle,
  Clock,
  Car,
  Settings
} from 'lucide-react';

interface QRScannerModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

interface Asset {
  id: string;
  code_tag: string;
  codeTag?: string;
  name: string;
  brand?: string;
  model: string;
  category: 'vehicle_light' | 'vehicle_heavy' | 'generator' | 'machinery' | string;
  criticality_level?: 'A' | 'B' | 'C' | string;
  criticalityLevel?: string;
  current_meter?: number;
  currentMeter?: number;
  meter_kind?: 'odometer_km' | 'hourmeter_hours' | string;
  meterKind?: string;
  status: 'operational' | 'breakdown' | 'maintenance' | string;
  cost_center: string;
  costCenter?: string;
  
  // Detalhes adicionais
  license_plate?: string;
  licensePlate?: string;
  plant_sector?: string;
  plantSector?: string;
  voltage_specs?: string;
  voltageSpecs?: string;
  power_kw_kva?: number;
  powerKwKva?: number;

  inatro_inspection_expiry?: string;
  inatroInspectionExpiry?: string;
  innoq_calibration_expiry?: string;
  innoqCalibrationExpiry?: string;
}

interface Document {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  document_type: string;
  documentType?: string;
  signed_url: string;
  signedUrl?: string;
  created_at: string;
}

export const QRScannerModal: React.FC<QRScannerModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  useEscapeKey(onClose, isOpen);
  const [scannerError, setScannerError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loadingAsset, setLoadingAsset] = useState(false);
  const [activeAsset, setActiveAsset] = useState<Asset | null>(null);
  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState<Document[]>([]);
  const [loadingDocs, setLoadingDocs] = useState(false);
  
  // Formulários de Campo
  const [meterValue, setMeterValue] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [submittingMeter, setSubmittingMeter] = useState(false);
  const [meterSuccess, setMeterSuccess] = useState(false);
  
  const [reportIssue, setReportIssue] = useState(false);
  const [issueTitle, setIssueTitle] = useState('');
  const [issueDescription, setReportIssueDescription] = useState('');
  const [issuePriority, setIssuePriority] = useState<'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'urgent'>('medium');
  const [issueMeterAtService, setIssueMeterAtService] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [submittingIssue, setSubmittingIssue] = useState(false);
  const [issueSuccess, setIssueSuccess] = useState(false);

  const scannerRef = useRef<Html5Qrcode | null>(null);
  const scannerId = "qr-reader-viewport";

  // Inicialização e Liberação da Câmera
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    // Reinicia os estados de ativo escaneado ao abrir
    setActiveAsset(null);
    setDocuments([]);
    setScannerError(null);
    setMeterValue('');
    setMeterSuccess(false);
    setReportIssue(false);
    setIssueTitle('');
    setReportIssueDescription('');
    setIssuePriority('medium');
    setIssueMeterAtService('');
    setIssueSuccess(false);

    // Pequeno atraso para garantir que o contêiner DOM esteja montado
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      startCamera();
    }, 400);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      stopCamera();
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  const startCamera = async () => {
    try {
      const html5Qrcode = new Html5Qrcode(scannerId);
      scannerRef.current = html5Qrcode;

      await html5Qrcode.start(
        { facingMode: "environment" },
        {
          fps: 10,
          qrbox: (width, height) => {
            const minEdge = Math.min(width, height);
            const boxSize = Math.floor(minEdge * 0.7);
            return { width: boxSize, height: boxSize };
          }
        },
        onScanSuccess,
        onScanFailure
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao iniciar câmera:", err);
      if (err.toString().includes("NotAllowedError") || err.toString().includes("Permission denied")) {
        setScannerError("Permissão de acesso à câmara foi negada. Por favor, ative as permissões nas configurações do navegador para escanear etiquetas.");
      } else {
        setScannerError("Não foi possível aceder à câmara traseira ou nenhuma câmara foi encontrada.");
      }
    }
  };

  const stopCamera = async () => {
    if (scannerRef.current && scannerRef.current.isScanning) {
      try {
        await scannerRef.current.stop();
        scannerRef.current.clear();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Erro ao parar câmera:", err);
      }
    }
    scannerRef.current = null;
  };

  const onScanSuccess = async (decodedText: string) => {
    // 1. Vibração tátil leve (se disponível)
    if (navigator.vibrate) {
      navigator.vibrate(100);
    }

    // 2. Para a câmera imediatamente para evitar múltiplos scans
    await stopCamera();

    // 3. Carrega o ativo escaneado
    loadAssetData(decodedText);
  };

  const onScanFailure = () => {
    // Ignoramos erros de frame individual para não poluir o console
  };

  const loadAssetData = async (assetId: string) => {
    setLoadingAsset(true);
    setScannerError(null);
    try {
      // Faz a requisição segura para obter o ativo pelo ID ou CodeTag
      const res = await api.get(`/assets/${encodeURIComponent(assetId)}`);
      const asset = res.data?.asset || res.data?.data || res.data;
      
      if (!asset || !asset.id) {
        throw new Error("Equipamento ou viatura não cadastrada no CMMS.");
      }

      setActiveAsset(asset);
      setMeterValue(asset.current_meter ?? asset.currentMeter ?? 0);
      setIssueMeterAtService(asset.current_meter ?? asset.currentMeter ?? 0);
      setIssuePriority('medium');
      setIssueTitle('');
      
      // Carrega os documentos desse ativo
      loadAssetDocs(asset.id);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao buscar ativo escaneado:", err);
      setScannerError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 
        err.message || 
        "Código QR lido com sucesso, mas o ativo correspondente não foi encontrado na base de dados real."
      );
      // Reinicia câmera para tentar novamente
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (isOpen && !activeAsset) {
          startCamera();
        }
      }, 5000);
    } finally {
      setLoadingAsset(false);
    }
  };

  const loadAssetDocs = async (id: string) => {
    setLoadingDocs(true);
    try {
      const res = await api.get(`/assets/${id}/documents`);
      const docs = res.data?.documents || res.data || [];
      setDocuments(docs);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Erro ao carregar documentos:", err);
    } finally {
      setLoadingDocs(false);
    }
  };

  // Enviar leitura do contador
  const handleUpdateMeter = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!activeAsset || meterValue === '') return;

    const current = activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0;
    if (Number(meterValue) < current) {
      alert(`O novo valor não pode ser menor do que o registo atual (${current}).`);
      return;
    }

    setSubmittingMeter(true);
    try {
      await api.patch(`/assets/${activeAsset.id}/meter`, {
        meterValue: Number(meterValue)
      });
      setMeterSuccess(true);
      
      // Atualiza o ativo local
      setActiveAsset(prev => {
        if (!prev) return null;
        return {
          ...prev,
          current_meter: Number(meterValue),
          currentMeter: Number(meterValue)
        };
      });
      
      setTimeout(() => setMeterSuccess(false), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao atualizar medidor:", err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || "Erro ao atualizar o contador no servidor.");
    } finally {
      setSubmittingMeter(false);
    }
  };

  // Reportar Avaria (Criar Ordem de Serviço Corretiva)
  const handleReportIssueSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!activeAsset || !issueTitle.trim() || !issueDescription.trim() || issueMeterAtService === '') return;

    setSubmittingIssue(true);
    try {
      const payload = {
        assetId: activeAsset.id,
        asset_id: activeAsset.id,
        title: issueTitle,
        description: issueDescription,
        orderType: 'corrective',
        order_type: 'corrective',
        priority: issuePriority,
        meterAtService: Number(issueMeterAtService),
        meter_at_service: Number(issueMeterAtService),
        status: 'open'
      };

      await api.post('/work-orders', payload);
      setIssueSuccess(true);
      setIssueTitle('');
      setReportIssueDescription('');
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        setIssueSuccess(false);
        setReportIssue(false);
      }, 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao reportar avaria:", err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || "Erro ao criar ordem de serviço corretiva.");
    } finally {
      setSubmittingIssue(false);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const isVehicle = activeAsset && (activeAsset.category === 'vehicle_light' || activeAsset.category === 'vehicle_heavy');
  const level = activeAsset?.criticality_level || activeAsset?.criticalityLevel || 'B';

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-lg rounded-md shadow-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        
        {/* Header do Scanner */}
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-white">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <QrCode className="text-blue-700" size={20} />
            <span className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-900">Leitor de QR Code Móvel</span>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              stopCamera();
              onClose();
            }}
            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Corpo do Modal */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">
          
          {/* MODO A: ESCANEANDO (Sem ativo selecionado) */}
          {!activeAsset && !loadingAsset && (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-6 space-y-6">
              
              {/* Moldura do Leitor QR */}
              <div className="relative w-72 h-72 border border-slate-300 rounded-md bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center">
                
                {/* Visualizador HTML5-QRCode */}
                <div id={scannerId} className="w-full h-full object-cover"></div>

                {/* Laser de Scan de Campo */}
                {!scannerError && (
                  <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 h-0.5 bg-blue-600 shadow-md shadow-blue-500/50 animate-laser-scanning z-10 pointer-events-none"></div>
                )}

                {/* Cantos Iluminados (Moldura Tecnológica) */}
                <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 w-6 h-6 border-t-2 border-l-2 border-blue-700 rounded-tl-sm pointer-events-none z-10"></div>
                <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 w-6 h-6 border-t-2 border-r-2 border-blue-700 rounded-tr-sm pointer-events-none z-10"></div>
                <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 w-6 h-6 border-b-2 border-l-2 border-blue-700 rounded-bl-sm pointer-events-none z-10"></div>
                <div className="absolute bottom-3 right-3 w-6 h-6 border-b-2 border-r-2 border-blue-700 rounded-br-sm pointer-events-none z-10"></div>
              </div>

              {/* Mensagem orientadora */}
              <div className="text-center px-4 max-w-sm">
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 font-medium leading-relaxed">
                  Aponte a câmara do telemóvel para a etiqueta QR Code colada na viatura ou gerador.
                </p>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1 uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold">
                  Abertura automática de ficha técnica
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Tratamento de Erros de Permissão ou Scanner */}
              {scannerError && (
                <div className="p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded-md flex gap-3 items-start max-w-sm">
                  <AlertTriangle size={18} className="text-red-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  <div className="flex-1 space-y-1">
                    <span className="font-bold block text-red-800">Falha no Scanner</span>
                    <span className="leading-relaxed block text-red-700">{scannerError}</span>
                    <button
                      onClick={startCamera}
                      className="mt-2 text-xs font-medium bg-white text-red-700 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border border-red-300 hover:bg-red-50 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      Tentar Novamente
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* MODO B: CARREGANDO DETALHES DO ATIVO */}
          {loadingAsset && (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 space-y-3">
              <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-blue-700" size={36} />
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium tracking-wide">A carregar detalhes de campo do ativo...</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* MODO C: PAINEL DE AÇÃO RÁPIDA DE CAMPO (Ativo Identificado!) */}
          {activeAsset && (
            <div className="space-y-4 animate-slide-up">
              
              {/* Header de Identificação do Ativo */}
              <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-xs text-blue-700 block tracking-wider uppercase">
                    {activeAsset.code_tag || activeAsset.codeTag}
                  </span>
                  <h3 className="text-slate-900 font-bold text-base leading-tight">{activeAsset.name}</h3>
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-600 block mt-0.5">{activeAsset.brand} {activeAsset.model}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                  <span className={`inline-flex px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium border ${
                    level === 'A' 
                      ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200' 
                      : level === 'B' 
                      ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200' 
                      : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200'
                  }`}>
