                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none font-mono placeholder-slate-400"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleRequestCloseModal}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 text-xs font-medium rounded-md transition-colors"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                {partModalMode === 'create' && hasFormInput() && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleSaveDraft(true)}
                    disabled={submittingPart}
                    className="px-4 py-2 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-xs font-medium rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    Salvar Rascunho
                  </button>
                )}
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submittingPart}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-md transition-all flex items-center gap-1 disabled:opacity-50 shadow-none"
                >
                  {submittingPart ? <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" /> : <span>Guardar Peça</span>}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
        </div>
      </SecondaryView>

      {/* ==========================================
          CAMADA SECUNDÁRIA: ENTRADA / SAÍDA RÁPIDA DE STOCK (REPOR STOCK)
          ========================================== */}
      <SecondaryView
        isOpen={Boolean(isStockModalOpen && selectedStockPart)}
        onClose={() => {
          setIsStockModalOpen(false);
          setSelectedStockPart(null);
        }}
        title={stockActionType === 'input' ? 'Entrada de Material no Armazém' : 'Saída para Consumo de Manutenção'}
        subtitle={selectedStockPart ? `${selectedStockPart.name} (${selectedStockPart.part_number || selectedStockPart.oem_reference || selectedStockPart.oemReference || 'S/N'})` : ''}
        icon={<ArrowRightLeft className="text-blue-700" size={18} />}
        maxWidth="max-w-xl"
      >
        {selectedStockPart && (
          <div className="p-6">
            <form onSubmit={handleStockUpdateSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-3 rounded-md text-xs space-y-1 text-slate-600">
                <div><span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Peça:</span> <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{selectedStockPart.name}</span></div>
                <div><span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">OEM / Ref:</span> <span className="font-mono text-blue-700 font-bold">{selectedStockPart.part_number || selectedStockPart.oem_reference || selectedStockPart.oemReference || 'S/N'}</span></div>
                <div><span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Localização:</span> <span className="text-slate-800">{selectedStockPart.location || selectedStockPart.warehouse_location || selectedStockPart.warehouseLocation || 'Geral'}</span></div>
                <div><span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Stock Disponível:</span> <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{selectedStockPart.current_stock ?? selectedStockPart.currentStock ?? 0} un</span></div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Operação Técnica</label>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setStockActionType('input')}
                    className={`py-2 text-xs font-medium rounded-md border transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ${
                      stockActionType === 'input'
                        ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-300 font-semibold'
                        : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <ArrowDownToLine size={13} />
                    <span>Entrada (Aumento)</span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setStockActionType('output')}
                    className={`py-2 text-xs font-medium rounded-md border transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ${
                      stockActionType === 'output'
                        ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-300 font-semibold'
                        : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <ArrowUpFromLine size={13} />
                    <span>Saída (Consumo)</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Quantidade *</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    required
                    min={1}
                    value={stockQuantity}
                    onChange={(e) => setStockQuantity(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                    placeholder="unidades"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none placeholder-slate-400"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Novo Custo / Uni (MZN) *</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    required
                    min={0}
                    value={stockUnitCost}
                    onChange={(e) => setStockUnitCost(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                    placeholder="MZN"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none placeholder-slate-400"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setIsStockModalOpen(false);
                    setSelectedStockPart(null);
                  }}
                  className="px-4 py-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 text-xs font-medium rounded-md"
                >
                  Voltar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submittingPart || stockQuantity === ''}
                  className={`px-4 py-1.5 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-md flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 shadow-none ${
                    stockActionType === 'input' ? 'bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800' : 'bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  {submittingPart ? <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" /> : <span>Confirmar</span>}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        )}
      </SecondaryView>

      {/* ==========================================
          CAMADA SECUNDÁRIA: CONFIRMAR ELIMINAÇÃO
          ========================================== */}
      <SecondaryView
        isOpen={Boolean(isDeleteModalOpen && deletingPart)}
        onClose={() => {
          setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
          setDeletingPart(null);
        }}
        title="Eliminar Artigo do Inventário"
        subtitle="Confirmação de exclusão definitiva de registro de peça"
        icon={<AlertTriangle size={18} className="text-red-600" />}
        maxWidth="max-w-xl"
      >
        {deletingPart && (
          <div className="p-6 space-y-4">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-red-50 text-red-600 border border-red-200 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center">
              <AlertTriangle size={24} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Eliminar Artigo do Inventário</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-1.5 leading-relaxed">
                Tem a certeza que deseja eliminar a peça <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 font-mono">{deletingPart.part_number || deletingPart.oem_reference || deletingPart.oemReference || 'S/N'}</span> - <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{deletingPart.name}</span>?
                Esta ação apagará as informações do armazém de forma irreversível.
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
                  setDeletingPart(null);
                }}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 text-xs font-medium rounded-md transition-all cursor-pointer"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleDeletePartConfirm}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-md transition-all shadow-none cursor-pointer"
              >
                Confirmar Eliminação
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </SecondaryView>

      <DraftConfirmModal
        isOpen={isDraftConfirmOpen}
        moduleName="Registo de Peça / Inventário"
        onSaveDraft={handleSaveDraft}
        onDiscard={handleDiscardDraft}
        onCancel={() => setIsDraftConfirmOpen(false)}
      />

    </div>
  );
};

export default InventoryPage;


=== FILE: src/pages/ItLogs.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import SecondaryView from '../components/SecondaryView';
import {
  Terminal,
  Search,
  RefreshCw,
  Loader2,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
  Play,
  Pause,
  CheckCircle,
  XCircle,
  Eye,
  Building2,
  User,
  Clock,
  Filter,
  CheckSquare,
  Square,
  Layers,
  ChevronRight,
  ShieldAlert,
  Calendar,
  AlertCircle
} from 'lucide-react';

export interface AuditLogItem {
  id: string;
  tenant_id?: string;
  tenant_name?: string;
  user_id?: string;
  user_email?: string;
  user_name?: string;
  action: string;
  details?: any;
  error_message?: string;
  log_type: 'success' | 'error';
  status: 'open' | 'start' | 'pause' | 'finished' | 'closed' | 'solved';
  is_read: boolean;
  is_opened: boolean;
  opened_at?: string;
  read_at?: string;
  created_at: string;
  tracking_history?: any[];
}

export const ItLogsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<AuditLogItem[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Filtros
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState<'all' | 'success' | 'error'>('all');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>('all');

  // Seleção múltipla
  const [selectedLogIds, setSelectedLogIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [batchUpdating, setBatchUpdating] = useState(false);

  // Modal de Detalhes
  const [selectedLog, setSelectedLog] = useState<AuditLogItem | null>(null);
  const [isDetailOpen, setIsDetailOpen] = useState(false);

  const fetchLogs = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const params: any = {};
      if (typeFilter !== 'all') params.logType = typeFilter;
      if (statusFilter !== 'all') params.status = statusFilter;
      if (searchQuery.trim()) params.search = searchQuery.trim();

      const response = await api.get('/it-logs', { params });
      const data = response.data?.logs || response.data?.data || response.data || [];
      setLogs(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar logs de IT:', err);
      setError('Não foi possível obter os logs de sistema.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchLogs();
  }, [typeFilter, statusFilter]);

  // Abertura e Leitura de Log
  const handleOpenLog = async (log: AuditLogItem) => {
    setSelectedLog(log);
    setIsDetailOpen(true);

    // Rastreamento: Marca no backend como aberto e lido
    try {
      await api.patch(`/it-logs/${log.id}/open`);
      await api.patch(`/it-logs/${log.id}/read`);
      // Atualiza localmente
      setLogs(prev =>
        prev.map(item =>
          item.id === log.id
            ? { ...item, is_opened: true, is_read: true, opened_at: new Date().toISOString(), read_at: new Date().toISOString() }
            : item
        )
      );
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Falha no rastreio de leitura do log:', e);
    }
  };

  // Alternar seleção de um log
  const toggleSelectLog = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedLogIds(prev =>
      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter(x => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
    );
  };

  // Alternar seleção de todos os logs filtrados
  const toggleSelectAll = () => {
    if (selectedLogIds.length === filteredLogs.length && filteredLogs.length > 0) {
      setSelectedLogIds([]);
    } else {
      setSelectedLogIds(filteredLogs.map(l => l.id));
    }
  };

  // Transição de Estado em Lote / Individual (start -> pause -> finished -> closed/solved)
  const handleBatchStatusTransition = async (newStatus: 'start' | 'pause' | 'finished' | 'closed') => {
    if (selectedLogIds.length === 0) return;
    setBatchUpdating(true);
    try {
      await api.patch('/it-logs/status', {
        logIds: selectedLogIds,
        status: newStatus
      });

      setSuccessMessage(`Estado dos ${selectedLogIds.length} logs alterado para "${newStatus.toUpperCase()}" com sucesso.`);
      setSelectedLogIds([]);
      fetchLogs();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3500);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao transitar estado dos logs:', err);
      alert('Não foi possível atualizar o estado dos logs selecionados.');
    } finally {
      setBatchUpdating(false);
    }
  };

  // Filtragem local por texto se já carregado
  const filteredLogs = logs.filter(l => {
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) return true;
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    const actionMatch = (l.action || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
    const userMatch = (l.user_email || l.user_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
    const tenantMatch = (l.tenant_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
    const errorMatch = (l.error_message || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
    return actionMatch || userMatch || tenantMatch || errorMatch;
  });

  const getStatusBadge = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'start':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200">
            <Play size={10} className="fill-current" />
            Em Análise (Start)
          </span>
        );
      case 'pause':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200">
            <Pause size={10} className="fill-current" />
            Em Pausa (Pause)
          </span>
        );
      case 'finished':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border border-indigo-200">
            <CheckCircle size={10} />
            Finalizado (Finished)
          </span>
        );
      case 'closed':
      case 'solved':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200">
            <CheckCircle2 size={10} />
            Resolvido (Closed)
          </span>
        );
      default:
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200">
            Pendente
          </span>
        );
    }
  };

  // Contadores
  const errorLogsCount = logs.filter(l => l.log_type === 'error').length;
  const inProgressCount = logs.filter(l => l.status === 'start' || l.status === 'pause').length;
  const solvedCount = logs.filter(l => l.status === 'closed' || l.status === 'solved' || l.status === 'finished').length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* CABEÇALHO */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <Terminal className="text-blue-700" size={24} />
            <span>Central de Logs de IT & Auditoria</span>
            {loading && <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin text-slate-400" />}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1 leading-relaxed">
            Monitorização operacional, diagnóstico de erros e encaminhamento de fluxos de suporte para todas as empresas e utilizadores ancorados.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={fetchLogs}
            disabled={loading}
            className="p-2.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold"
            title="Atualizar Logs"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={15} className={loading ? 'animate-spin text-blue-700' : ''} />
            <span>Atualizar</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* FEEDBACK DE SUCESSO */}
      {successMessage && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-xs rounded-md flex gap-3 items-center">
          <CheckCircle2 size={18} className="text-emerald-700 shrink-0" />
          <span className="font-semibold">{successMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded-md flex gap-3 items-center">
          <AlertCircle size={18} className="text-red-700 shrink-0" />
          <span>{error}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* CARDS DE RESUMO OPERACIONAL */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md shadow-none flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Terminal size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase block">Total de Eventos</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">{logs.length}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md shadow-none flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-red-50 text-red-600 border border-red-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <AlertTriangle size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase block">Erros Detectados</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold text-red-600">{errorLogsCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md shadow-none flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Play size={18} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase block">Em Resolução (IT)</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold text-blue-700">{inProgressCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md shadow-none flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <CheckCircle2 size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase block">Solucionados / Fechados</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold text-emerald-700">{solvedCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* FILTROS E PESQUISA */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-3 items-center justify-between shadow-none">
        <div className="relative w-full md:max-w-md">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400" size={16} />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Pesquisar por ação, utilizador, empresa ou mensagem de erro..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:bg-white rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none transition-colors"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5 w-full md:w-auto">
          {/* Filtro por Tipo */}
          <select
            value={typeFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setTypeFilter(e.target.value as any)}
            className="bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <option value="all">Todos os Tipos (Sucesso e Erros)</option>
            <option value="error">Apenas Falhas / Erros</option>
            <option value="success">Apenas Sucessos</option>
          </select>

          {/* Filtro por Estado */}
          <select
            value={statusFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <option value="all">Todos os Estados</option>
            <option value="open">Pendente (Open)</option>
            <option value="start">Em Análise (Start)</option>
            <option value="pause">Em Pausa (Pause)</option>
            <option value="finished">Finalizado (Finished)</option>
            <option value="closed">Fechado / Solucionado (Closed)</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* BARRA DE AÇÕES PARA SELEÇÃO MÚLTIPLA */}
      {selectedLogIds.length > 0 && (
        <div className="p-3 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-md flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 animate-fade-in shadow-none">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-900">
            <CheckSquare size={16} className="text-blue-700" />
            <span>{selectedLogIds.length} {selectedLogIds.length === 1 ? 'log selecionado' : 'logs selecionados'}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-600 mr-1">Encaminhar fluxo:</span>
            
            <button
              onClick={() => handleBatchStatusTransition('start')}
              disabled={batchUpdating}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-blue-600 hover:text-white border border-blue-300 text-blue-700 rounded text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              title="Iniciar tratamento do log"
            >
              <Play size={12} />
              <span>Iniciar (Start)</span>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={() => handleBatchStatusTransition('pause')}
              disabled={batchUpdating}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-amber-600 hover:text-white border border-amber-300 text-amber-700 rounded text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              title="Colocar log em pausa"
            >
              <Pause size={12} />
              <span>Pausar (Pause)</span>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={() => handleBatchStatusTransition('finished')}
              disabled={batchUpdating}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-indigo-600 hover:text-white border border-indigo-300 text-indigo-700 rounded text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              title="Marcar como finalizado pela equipa técnica"
            >
              <CheckCircle size={12} />
              <span>Finalizar (Finished)</span>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={() => handleBatchStatusTransition('closed')}
              disabled={batchUpdating}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              title="Fechar e considerar solucionado"
            >
              <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
              <span>Fechar / Solucionado</span>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={() => setSelectedLogIds([])}
              className="px-2 py-1 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 text-xs font-medium cursor-pointer"
            >
              Desmarcar
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* TABELA DE LOGS */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="p-16 text-center bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md">
          <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-blue-700 mx-auto" size={32} />
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-3">A carregar registos de IT...</p>
        </div>
      ) : filteredLogs.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="p-16 text-center bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md">
          <Terminal size={40} className="text-slate-300 mx-auto mb-3" />
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">Nenhum Log Encontrado</h3>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">
            Não existem registos de auditoria correspondentes aos critérios de pesquisa e filtros selecionados.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md overflow-hidden shadow-none">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-600 font-semibold uppercase text-[11px]">
                  <th className="py-3 px-3 w-10 text-center">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={toggleSelectAll}
                      className="text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 cursor-pointer"
                      title="Selecionar todos os registos filtrados"
                    >
                      {selectedLogIds.length > 0 && selectedLogIds.length === filteredLogs.length ? (
                        <CheckSquare size={16} className="text-blue-700" />
                      ) : (
                        <Square size={16} />
                      )}
                    </button>
                  </th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-3">Estado</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-3">Tipo</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Ação / Descrição</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Empresa (Tenant)</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Utilizador Ancorado</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-3">Rastreio (Aberto / Lido)</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-3">Data / Hora</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-3 text-right">Ação</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {filteredLogs.map((log) => {
                  const isSelected = selectedLogIds.includes(log.id);
                  const isError = log.log_type === 'error';

                  return (
                    <tr
                      key={log.id}
                      className={`hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors ${
                        isSelected ? 'bg-blue-50/50' : isError ? 'bg-red-50/20' : ''
                      }`}
                    >
                      {/* Checkbox de seleção */}
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 text-center">
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          checked={isSelected}
                          onChange={() => toggleSelectLog(log.id)}
                          className="w-4 h-4 rounded text-blue-600 border-slate-300 cursor-pointer focus:ring-0"
                        />
                      </td>

                      {/* Estado do Log no Fluxo */}
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {getStatusBadge(log.status)}
                      </td>

                      {/* Tipo: Sucesso ou Erro */}
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {isError ? (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-red-700 font-semibold">
                            <XCircle size={14} className="text-red-600" />
                            Erro
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-700 font-semibold">
                            <CheckCircle2 size={14} className="text-emerald-600" />
                            Sucesso
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      {/* Ação */}
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-slate-900 max-w-xs truncate" title={log.action}>
                        {log.action}
                        {log.error_message && (
                          <span className="block text-[11px] font-normal text-red-600 truncate mt-0.5">
                            {log.error_message}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      {/* Empresa */}
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-slate-700">
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-medium">
                          <Building2 size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
                          {log.tenant_name || 'Avexas Global'}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      {/* Utilizador */}
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-slate-700">
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-[11px]">
                          <User size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
                          {log.user_email || log.user_name || 'Sistema Automático'}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      {/* Rastreio: Aberto / Lido */}
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                        <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 text-[10px]">
                          {log.is_opened ? (
                            <span className="text-emerald-700 font-medium">✓ Aberto</span>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-slate-400">Não aberto</span>
                          )}
                          {log.is_read ? (
                            <span className="text-blue-700 font-medium">✓ Lido</span>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-slate-400">Não lido</span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      {/* Data / Hora */}
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 text-slate-500 font-mono text-[11px] whitespace-nowrap">
                        {new Date(log.created_at).toLocaleString('pt-MZ')}
                      </td>

                      {/* Botão de Abrir */}
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleOpenLog(log)}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded text-xs font-medium flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors shadow-none"
                        >
                          <Eye size={12} />
                          <span>Abrir</span>
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* CAMADA SECUNDÁRIA: DETALHES COMPLETOS DO LOG */}
      <SecondaryView
        isOpen={isDetailOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsDetailOpen(false)}
        title={selectedLog ? `Detalhes do Log: ${selectedLog.action}` : 'Detalhes do Log'}
        subtitle="Rastreabilidade completa de execução, metadados e histórico de tratamento"
        icon={<Terminal className="text-blue-700" size={18} />}
        maxWidth="max-w-2xl"
      >
        {selectedLog && (
          <div className="p-6 space-y-5">
            {/* Cabeçalho do Log */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-4 border-b border-slate-100">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {getStatusBadge(selectedLog.status)}
                {selectedLog.log_type === 'error' ? (
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-red-100 text-red-800">
                    Falha Operacional
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800">
                    Execução com Sucesso
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono">
                {new Date(selectedLog.created_at).toLocaleString('pt-MZ')}
              </span>
            </div>

            {/* Metadados de Ancoragem */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs">
              <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded border border-slate-200">
                <span className="text-slate-500 block text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold">Empresa Ancorada</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 mt-1 block">
                  {selectedLog.tenant_name || selectedLog.tenant_id || 'Avexas Global'}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded border border-slate-200">
                <span className="text-slate-500 block text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold">Utilizador Ancorado</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 mt-1 block font-mono">
                  {selectedLog.user_email || selectedLog.user_name || 'Sistema'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Informação de Rastreio */}
            <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded border border-slate-200 text-xs space-y-1">
              <span className="text-slate-500 block text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold">Rastreamento de Auditoria</span>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-slate-700 pt-1">
                <span>Data de Abertura:</span>
                <span className="font-mono">{selectedLog.opened_at ? new Date(selectedLog.opened_at).toLocaleString('pt-MZ') : 'Agora mesmo'}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-slate-700">
                <span>Data de Leitura:</span>
                <span className="font-mono">{selectedLog.read_at ? new Date(selectedLog.read_at).toLocaleString('pt-MZ') : 'Agora mesmo'}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Mensagem de Erro se houver */}
            {selectedLog.error_message && (
              <div>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-red-700 block mb-1">Mensagem de Erro / Stack:</span>
                <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 p-3 rounded text-red-800 font-mono text-xs overflow-x-auto whitespace-pre-wrap">
                  {selectedLog.error_message}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Payload / Detalhes Estruturados */}
            <div>
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Carga de Dados (Payload / Contexto):</span>
              <pre className="bg-slate-900 text-slate-100 p-4 rounded text-xs font-mono overflow-x-auto max-h-60">
                {JSON.stringify(selectedLog.details || {}, null, 2)}
              </pre>
            </div>

            {/* Ações de Estado sobre o Log Aberto */}
            <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-200">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 block mb-2">Transição de Estado Operacional:</span>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={async () => {
                    await api.patch('/it-logs/status', { logIds: [selectedLog.id], status: 'start' });
                    setSelectedLog({ ...selectedLog, status: 'start' });
                    fetchLogs();
                  }}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-blue-600 hover:text-white border border-blue-300 text-blue-700 rounded text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                >
                  <Play size={12} />
                  <span>Iniciar (Start)</span>
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={async () => {
                    await api.patch('/it-logs/status', { logIds: [selectedLog.id], status: 'pause' });
                    setSelectedLog({ ...selectedLog, status: 'pause' });
                    fetchLogs();
                  }}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-amber-600 hover:text-white border border-amber-300 text-amber-700 rounded text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                >
                  <Pause size={12} />
                  <span>Pausar (Pause)</span>
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={async () => {
                    await api.patch('/it-logs/status', { logIds: [selectedLog.id], status: 'finished' });
                    setSelectedLog({ ...selectedLog, status: 'finished' });
                    fetchLogs();
                  }}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-indigo-600 hover:text-white border border-indigo-300 text-indigo-700 rounded text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                >
                  <CheckCircle size={12} />
                  <span>Finalizar (Finished)</span>
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={async () => {
                    await api.patch('/it-logs/status', { logIds: [selectedLog.id], status: 'closed' });
                    setSelectedLog({ ...selectedLog, status: 'closed' });
                    fetchLogs();
                  }}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                >
                  <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
                  <span>Fechar / Solucionado</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </SecondaryView>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ItLogsPage;


=== FILE: src/pages/Login.tsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  RefreshCw,
  Eye,
  EyeOff
} from 'lucide-react';

export const LoginPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [capsLockActive, setCapsLockActive] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !password) return;

    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const loggedUser = await login(email, password);
      
      // Redirecionamento condicional baseado no papel (role) do utilizador
      if (loggedUser.role === 'super_admin') {
        navigate('/super-admin');
      } else {
        navigate('/app/dashboard');
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro durante o login:', err);
      const message = err.message || 'Ocorreu um erro ao conectar ao servidor. Verifique a sua ligação à internet.';
      setError(message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div 
      className="min-h-screen w-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 relative"
      style={{
        backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC',
        backgroundImage: 'radial-gradient(#CBD5E1 1px, transparent 1px)',
        backgroundSize: '24px 24px'
      }}
    >
      
      {/* Logótipo FORA do Cartão */}
      <div className="mb-6 flex justify-center z-10">
        <img
          src="https://i.postimg.cc/NfvHTQTR/avexas-logo-transp-copy.png"
          alt="Avexas CMMS"
          draggable={false}
          onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
          onDragStart={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
          className="h-10 w-auto object-contain select-none pointer-events-none"
          style={{ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none', WebkitTouchCallout: 'none' }}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Cartão do Formulário Compacto */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-sm w-full max-w-sm p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-4 z-10">
        
        {/* Banner de Alerta de Erro */}
        {error && (
          <div className="p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded-lg flex gap-2.5 items-start animate-fade-in">
            <AlertTriangle size={16} className="shrink-0 text-red-600 mt-0.5" />
            <div className="flex-1">
              <span className="font-bold block text-red-800">Falha na Autenticação</span>
              <span className="leading-relaxed text-red-700">{error}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="email" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              E-mail
            </label>
            <input
              id="email"
              type="email"
              required
              disabled={loading}
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-sm rounded-lg border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] outline-none transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="password" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              Senha
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <input
                id="password"
                type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                required
                disabled={loading}
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => setCapsLockActive(e.getModifierState('CapsLock'))}
                onKeyUp={(e) => setCapsLockActive(e.getModifierState('CapsLock'))}
                className="w-full h-10 pl-3 pr-10 text-sm rounded-lg border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] outline-none transition-all"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                aria-label={showPassword ? 'Ocultar senha' : 'Exibir senha'}
              >
                {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={16} /> : <Eye size={16} />}
              </button>
            </div>
            {capsLockActive && (
              <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-600 font-medium flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                ⇪ Fixador de Maiúsculas (Caps Lock) ativado
              </span>
            )}
          </div>


          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
            className="w-full h-10 bg-[#0066FF] hover:bg-[#0052CC] text-white font-semibold text-sm rounded-lg transition-colors shadow-none flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loading ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <RefreshCw size={16} className="animate-spin" />
                <span>Acessar...</span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <span>Acessar</span>
            )}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default LoginPage;


=== FILE: src/pages/MaintenancePlans.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useCurrentDomain } from '../config/domainRegistry';
import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';
import SecondaryView from '../components/SecondaryView';
import { triggerGlobalConfirm } from '../contexts/AlertContext';
import { dataCache } from '../services/dataCacheService';
import {
  CalendarClock,
  Plus,
  RefreshCw,
  Loader2,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Play,
  Pause,
  Edit2,
  Trash2,
  Zap,
  CheckSquare,
  Search,
  Wrench,
  Clock,
  Layers,
  X,
  User,
  AlertCircle,
  Save
} from 'lucide-react';
import { draftService, DraftItem } from '../services/draftService';
import { DraftConfirmModal } from '../components/DraftConfirmModal';
import { DraftBanner } from '../components/DraftBanner';
import { DraftsListView } from '../components/DraftsListView';

export interface Asset {
  id: string;
  code_tag?: string;
  codeTag?: string;
  name: string;
  operational_scope?: string;
  operationalScope?: string;
}

export interface Technician {
  id: string;
  name?: string;
  fullName?: string;
  full_name?: string;
  email?: string;
  specialty?: string;
  role?: string;
  operational_scope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both' | 'mixed';
}

export interface ChecklistItem {
  id: string;
  task: string;
}

export interface MaintenancePlan {
  id: string;
  code?: string;
  title: string;
  asset_id: string;
  asset_name?: string;
  assetName?: string;
  asset_code?: string;
  assetCode?: string;
  trigger_type: 'time' | 'meter' | 'both';
  triggerType?: 'time' | 'meter' | 'both';
  interval_days?: number;
  intervalDays?: number;
  interval_meter?: number;
  intervalMeter?: number;
  priority?: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'critical';
  assigned_technician_id?: string;
  assignedTechnicianId?: string;
  technician_name?: string;
  technicianName?: string;
  checklist_items?: string[] | ChecklistItem[];
  checklist?: ChecklistItem[];
  is_active?: boolean;
  isActive?: boolean;
  status?: 'active' | 'paused';
  next_due_date?: string;
  nextDueDate?: string;
  next_due_meter?: number;
  nextDueMeter?: number;
  created_at?: string;
}

export const MaintenancePlansPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, isTechnician, currentScope } = useAuth();
  const domainConfig = useCurrentDomain();
  const { domain } = domainConfig;

  // Estados sem qualquer dado mockado
  const [plans, setPlans] = useState<MaintenancePlan[]>(() => dataCache.get<MaintenancePlan[]>('maintenance_plans') || []);
  const [assets, setAssets] = useState<Asset[]>([]);
  const [technicians, setTechnicians] = useState<Technician[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(() => !dataCache.has('maintenance_plans'));
  const [loadingFormData, setLoadingFormData] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Filtros e Notificações
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Modal de Criação / Edição
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [editingPlan, setEditingPlan] = useState<MaintenancePlan | null>(null);

  // Estados de Rascunho (Drafts)
  const [hasRestoredDraft, setHasRestoredDraft] = useState(false);
  const [draftSavedAt, setDraftSavedAt] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [isDraftConfirmOpen, setIsDraftConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
  const [planDrafts, setPlanDrafts] = useState<DraftItem[]>([]);

  const loadDrafts = () => {
    const list = draftService.getDrafts('maintenance_plans', user?.tenantId || (user as any)?.tenant_id, user?.id);
    setPlanDrafts(list);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDrafts();
    const handleDraftsUpdated = (e: any) => {
      if (!e.detail || e.detail.module === 'maintenance_plans') {
        loadDrafts();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('cmms:drafts-updated', handleDraftsUpdated);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('cmms:drafts-updated', handleDraftsUpdated);
  }, [user]);

  const handleResumeDraft = (draft: DraftItem) => {
    const data = draft.data || {};
    setTitle(data.title || '');
    setAssetId(data.assetId || '');
    setTriggerType(data.triggerType || 'meter');
    setIntervalDays(data.intervalDays || 90);
    setIntervalMeter(data.intervalMeter || 10000);
    setPriority(data.priority || 'medium');
    setAssignedTechnicianId(data.assignedTechnicianId || '');
    if (data.checklist && Array.isArray(data.checklist)) {
      setChecklist(data.checklist);
    }
    setHasRestoredDraft(true);
    setDraftSavedAt(draft.timestamp);
    setEditingPlan(null);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteDraft = (draftId: string) => {
    draftService.deleteDraft('maintenance_plans', draftId, user?.tenantId || (user as any)?.tenant_id, user?.id);
    loadDrafts();
  };

  // Campos do Formulário
  const [title, setTitle] = useState<string>('');
  const [assetId, setAssetId] = useState<string>('');
  const [triggerType, setTriggerType] = useState<'time' | 'meter' | 'both'>('meter');
  const [intervalDays, setIntervalDays] = useState<number>(90);
  const [intervalMeter, setIntervalMeter] = useState<number>(10000);
  const [priority, setPriority] = useState<'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'critical'>('medium');
  const [assignedTechnicianId, setAssignedTechnicianId] = useState<string>('');
  const [checklist, setChecklist] = useState<ChecklistItem[]>([
    { id: '1', task: 'Verificar nível de óleo e filtros principais' },
    { id: '2', task: 'Inspecionar componentes de segurança e aperto geral' }
  ]);
  const [newTaskInput, setNewTaskInput] = useState<string>('');

  // 1. Consumo de dados reais da API (/maintenance-plans)
  const fetchPlans = async (force: boolean | unknown = false) => {
    const isForce = force === true;
    if (isForce) {
      dataCache.invalidate('maintenance_plans');
    }
    if (!dataCache.has('maintenance_plans') || isForce) {
      setLoading(true);
    }
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/maintenance-plans');
      const data = Array.isArray(res.data)
        ? res.data
        : (res.data?.plans || res.data?.data || []);
      setPlans(data);
      dataCache.set('maintenance_plans', data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar planos preventivos:', err);
      setPlans([]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!dataCache.has('maintenance_plans')) {
      fetchPlans();
    }
  }, []);

  // 2. Carrega dependências reais do Modal (Ativos e Técnicos)
  const loadModalDependencies = async () => {
    setLoadingFormData(true);
    try {
      const [assetsRes, techsRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
        api.get('/assets'),
        api.get('/technicians')
      ]);

      if (assetsRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
        const aData = Array.isArray(assetsRes.value.data)
          ? assetsRes.value.data
          : (assetsRes.value.data?.assets || assetsRes.value.data?.data || []);
        
        // Filtrar ativos do domínio ativo se especificado
        const domainAssets = aData.filter((a: any) => {
          if (domain === 'plant') {
            return a.operational_scope === 'plant' || a.operationalScope === 'plant';
          }
          if (domain === 'fleet') {
            return a.operational_scope === 'fleet' || a.operationalScope === 'fleet';
          }
          return true;
        });

        const activeAssetsList = domainAssets.length > 0 ? domainAssets : aData;
        setAssets(activeAssetsList);

        if (activeAssetsList.length > 0 && !assetId) {
          setAssetId(activeAssetsList[0].id);
        }
      }

      if (techsRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
        const tData = Array.isArray(techsRes.value.data)
          ? techsRes.value.data
          : (techsRes.value.data?.technicians || techsRes.value.data?.data || []);
        const uniqueTechs = Array.from(
          new Map<string, Technician>(tData.map((t: Technician) => {
            const key = t.email?.toLowerCase() || t.name?.toLowerCase() || t.fullName?.toLowerCase() || t.full_name?.toLowerCase() || t.id;
            return [key, t];
          })).values()
        );
        setTechnicians(uniqueTechs);

        if (uniqueTechs.length > 0 && !assignedTechnicianId) {
          setAssignedTechnicianId(uniqueTechs[0].id);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar dados do formulário:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoadingFormData(false);
    }
  };

  const handleOpenCreateModal = () => {
    setEditingPlan(null);
    const draft = draftService.getDraft<any>('maintenance_plans', user?.tenantId || (user as any)?.tenant_id, user?.id);
    if (draft && draft.data) {
      setTitle(draft.data.title || '');
      setAssetId(draft.data.assetId || '');
      setTriggerType(draft.data.triggerType || 'meter');
      setIntervalDays(draft.data.intervalDays || 90);
      setIntervalMeter(draft.data.intervalMeter || 10000);
      setPriority(draft.data.priority || 'medium');
      setAssignedTechnicianId(draft.data.assignedTechnicianId || '');
      if (Array.isArray(draft.data.checklist)) {
        setChecklist(draft.data.checklist);
      }
      setHasRestoredDraft(true);
      setDraftSavedAt(draft.timestamp);
    } else {
      setTitle('');
      setTriggerType('meter');
      setIntervalDays(90);
      setIntervalMeter(10000);
      setPriority('medium');
      setChecklist([
        { id: '1', task: 'Verificar nível de óleo e filtros de substituição' },
        { id: '2', task: 'Inspeção técnica dos travões e fixações mecânicas' }
      ]);
      setHasRestoredDraft(false);
      setDraftSavedAt(null);
    }
    setIsModalOpen(true);
    loadModalDependencies();
  };

  const handleRequestCloseModal = () => {
    if (editingPlan) {
      setIsModalOpen(false);
      return;
    }
    const hasData = title.trim() !== '' || (checklist.length > 2 || (checklist.length > 0 && checklist[0].task !== 'Verificar nível de óleo e filtros de substituição'));
    if (hasData) {
      setIsDraftConfirmOpen(true);
    } else {
      setIsModalOpen(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveDraft = () => {
    const planTitle = title.trim() || 'Novo Plano Preventivo';
    const subtitle = `Gatilho: ${triggerType === 'both' ? 'Dias e Km' : triggerType === 'time' ? `${intervalDays} dias` : `${intervalMeter} km`}`;
    draftService.saveDraft('maintenance_plans', {
      title,
      assetId,
      triggerType,
      intervalDays,
      intervalMeter,
      priority,
      assignedTechnicianId,
      checklist
    }, user?.tenantId || (user as any)?.tenant_id, user?.id, { title: planTitle, subtitle });
    setIsDraftConfirmOpen(false);
    setIsModalOpen(false);
    loadDrafts();
    setSuccessMessage('Rascunho de plano preventivo guardado com sucesso na lista de rascunhos!');
    setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
  };

  const handleDiscardDraft = () => {
    draftService.clearDraft('maintenance_plans', user?.tenantId || (user as any)?.tenant_id, user?.id);
    setIsDraftConfirmOpen(false);
    setIsModalOpen(false);
    setHasRestoredDraft(false);
    loadDrafts();
  };

  const handleDiscardRestoredDraft = () => {
    draftService.clearDraft('maintenance_plans', user?.tenantId || (user as any)?.tenant_id, user?.id);
    setTitle('');
    setTriggerType('meter');
    setIntervalDays(90);
    setIntervalMeter(10000);
    setPriority('medium');
    setChecklist([
      { id: '1', task: 'Verificar nível de óleo e filtros de substituição' },
      { id: '2', task: 'Inspeção técnica dos travões e fixações mecânicas' }
    ]);
    setHasRestoredDraft(false);
  };

  // Tecla ESC com verificação de rascunhos
  useEscapeKey(handleRequestCloseModal, isModalOpen && !isDraftConfirmOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsDraftConfirmOpen(false), isDraftConfirmOpen);

  const handleOpenEditModal = (plan: MaintenancePlan) => {
    setEditingPlan(plan);
    setTitle(plan.title || '');
    setAssetId(plan.asset_id || '');
    setTriggerType(plan.trigger_type || plan.triggerType || 'meter');
    setIntervalDays(plan.interval_days || plan.intervalDays || 90);
    setIntervalMeter(plan.interval_meter || plan.intervalMeter || 10000);
    setPriority(plan.priority || 'medium');
    setAssignedTechnicianId(plan.assigned_technician_id || plan.assignedTechnicianId || '');

    // Formatar checklist
    if (Array.isArray(plan.checklist)) {
      setChecklist(plan.checklist);
    } else if (Array.isArray(plan.checklist_items)) {
      const formatted = plan.checklist_items.map((item: any, idx) =>
        typeof item === 'string' ? { id: String(idx + 1), task: item } : item
      );
      setChecklist(formatted);
    } else {
      setChecklist([]);
    }

    setIsModalOpen(true);
    loadModalDependencies();
  };

  const handleAddTask = () => {
    if (!newTaskInput.trim()) return;
    setChecklist([
      ...checklist,
      { id: String(Date.now()), task: newTaskInput.trim() }
    ]);
    setNewTaskInput('');
  };

  const handleRemoveTask = (id: string) => {
    setChecklist(checklist.filter(item => item.id !== id));
  };

  // 3. Salvar Plano via API (POST / PUT /maintenance-plans)
  const handleSavePlan = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title || !assetId) return;

    const payload = {
      title,
      asset_id: assetId,
      trigger_type: triggerType,
      interval_days: Number(intervalDays),
      interval_meter: Number(intervalMeter),
      priority,
      assigned_technician_id: assignedTechnicianId || undefined,
      checklist_items: checklist.map(c => c.task)
    };

    try {
      if (editingPlan) {
        await api.put(`/maintenance-plans/${editingPlan.id}`, payload);
        setSuccessMessage('Plano preventivo atualizado com sucesso!');
      } else {
        await api.post('/maintenance-plans', payload);
        setSuccessMessage('Novo plano de manutenção preventivo criado com sucesso!');
      }
      draftService.clearDraft('maintenance_plans', user?.tenantId || (user as any)?.tenant_id, user?.id);
      setIsModalOpen(false);
      fetchPlans(true);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao salvar plano preventivo:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Erro ao comunicar com a API ao guardar o plano.');
    }
  };

  // 4. Alternar Ativo/Pausado via API (PATCH /maintenance-plans/:id/toggle)
  const handleToggleActive = async (plan: MaintenancePlan) => {
    try {
      try {
        await api.patch(`/maintenance-plans/${plan.id}/toggle`);
      } catch {
        const currentActive = plan.is_active ?? plan.status === 'active';
        await api.patch(`/maintenance-plans/${plan.id}`, { is_active: !currentActive, status: !currentActive ? 'active' : 'paused' });
      }
      fetchPlans(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao alterar estado do plano:', err);
    }
  };

  // 5. Gerar OS Preventiva Imediata via API (POST /maintenance-plans/:id/generate-wo)
  const handleGenerateWO = async (plan: MaintenancePlan) => {
    try {
      try {
        await api.post(`/maintenance-plans/${plan.id}/generate-wo`);
      } catch {
        // Fallback direto via POST /work-orders
        await api.post('/work-orders', {
          title: `[Preventiva] ${plan.title}`,
          asset_id: plan.asset_id,
          type: 'preventive',
          priority: plan.priority || 'medium',
          description: `Ordem de Serviço Preventiva gerada a partir do plano ${plan.code || plan.id}.\n\nChecklist:\n` +
            (Array.isArray(plan.checklist)
              ? plan.checklist.map((c, i) => `${i + 1}. [ ] ${c.task}`).join('\n')
              : 'Verificação periódica')
        });
      }
      setSuccessMessage(`Ordem de Serviço Preventiva gerada com sucesso!`);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao gerar Ordem de Serviço Preventiva:', err);
      alert('Erro ao gerar Ordem de Serviço na API.');
    }
  };

  // 6. Eliminar Plano via API (DELETE /maintenance-plans/:id)
  const handleDeletePlan = async (id: string) => {
    const confirmed = await triggerGlobalConfirm({
      title: 'Eliminar Plano Preventivo',
      message: 'Tem a certeza que deseja eliminar definitivamente este plano preventivo?',
      confirmText: 'Sim, Eliminar',
      cancelText: 'Cancelar',
      danger: true
    });
    if (!confirmed) return;
    try {
      await api.delete(`/maintenance-plans/${id}`);
      setSuccessMessage('Plano preventivo eliminado com sucesso.');
      fetchPlans(true);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao eliminar plano:', err);
      alert('Erro ao eliminar plano na API.');
    }
  };

  // Indicadores do Topo Calculados em Tempo Real (Zero Números Fixos)
  const activePlansCount = plans.filter(p => p.is_active ?? p.status === 'active').length;
  const pausedPlansCount = plans.filter(p => (p.is_active === false) || p.status === 'paused').length;

  const upcomingCount = plans.filter(p => {
    const isActive = p.is_active ?? p.status === 'active';
    const dueDate = p.next_due_date || p.nextDueDate;
    if (!dueDate || !isActive) return false;
    const diffDays = Math.ceil((new Date(dueDate).getTime() - new Date().getTime()) / (1000 * 3600 * 24));
    return diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 15;
  }).length;

  // Filtros
  const filteredPlans = plans.filter(plan => {
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    const assetName = plan.asset_name || plan.assetName || '';
    const code = plan.code || '';

    const matchesSearch =
      !searchQuery ||
      plan.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      code.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      assetName.toLowerCase().includes(q);

    const isActive = plan.is_active ?? plan.status === 'active';
    const matchesStatus =
      statusFilter === 'all' ||
      (statusFilter === 'active' && isActive) ||
      (statusFilter === 'paused' && !isActive);

    return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
  });

  const availableTechnicians = technicians.filter((tech) =>
    !tech.operational_scope ||
    tech.operational_scope === 'both' ||
    tech.operational_scope === 'mixed' ||
    tech.operational_scope === currentScope
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      
      {/* TOAST DE SUCESSO */}
      {successMessage && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 p-4 rounded-md shadow-lg max-w-md flex gap-3 items-start animate-fade-in">
          <CheckCircle2 className="text-emerald-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" size={18} />
          <div>
            <span className="font-bold block text-sm text-slate-900">Operação Concluída</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-600">{successMessage}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* CABEÇALHO */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <span>Plano de Manutenção</span>
            {loading && <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin text-slate-400" />}
          </h1>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
          <button
            onClick={fetchPlans}
            disabled={loading}
            className="p-2.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-all cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            title="Recarregar"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={16} className={loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''} />
          </button>

          {!isTechnician && (
            <button
              onClick={handleOpenCreateModal}
              className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 active:bg-blue-900 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2.5 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none"
            >
              <Plus size={16} />
              <span>Novo Plano Preventivo</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* MÉTRICAS RÁPIDAS NO TOPO (3 CARDS CALCULADOS EM TEMPO REAL) */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-none">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <CheckCircle2 size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block">Planos Ativos</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5 block">{loading ? '---' : activePlansCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-none">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Clock size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block">Revisões Próximas (15 dias)</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5 block">{loading ? '---' : upcomingCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-none">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Pause size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block">Planos Pausados</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5 block">{loading ? '---' : pausedPlansCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* BARRA DE PESQUISA E FILTROS */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 space-y-3 shadow-none">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div className="relative">
            <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400" size={16} />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Pesquisar plano por código, título ou ativo..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600 focus:bg-white"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <select
              value={statusFilter}
              onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600 focus:bg-white cursor-pointer"
            >
              <option value="all">Todos os Estados</option>
              <option value="active">Ativos</option>
              <option value="paused">Pausados</option>
              <option value="drafts">Rascunhos Salvos ({planDrafts.length})</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* TABELA DE PLANOS PREVENTIVOS OU LISTA DE RASCUNHOS */}
      {statusFilter === 'drafts' ? (
        <DraftsListView
          moduleName="Plano de Manutenção"
          drafts={planDrafts}
          onResumeDraft={handleResumeDraft}
          onDeleteDraft={handleDeleteDraft}
          onNewItem={() => handleOpenCreateModal()}
        />
      ) : (
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md overflow-hidden shadow-none">
        {loading ? (
          <div className="p-12 text-center">
            <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-blue-700 mx-auto" size={32} />
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-3">A carregar planos preventivos da API...</p>
          </div>
        ) : plans.length === 0 ? (
          /* ESTADO VAZIO REAL (EMPTY STATE) */
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-12 text-center">
            <CalendarClock size={36} className="text-slate-300 mx-auto mb-3" />
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">Nenhum Plano de Manutenção Preventiva</h3>
          </div>
        ) : filteredPlans.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-12 text-center">
            <Search className="text-slate-300 mx-auto" size={36} />
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 mt-2">Nenhum plano encontrado</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">
              Tente ajustar os critérios de pesquisa ou limpar os filtros.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-xs border-collapse">
              <thead className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-600 uppercase font-semibold text-[10px] tracking-wider">
                <tr>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Código & Título</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Ativo Alvo</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Periodicidade / Gatilho</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Checklist</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Estado</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Ações</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-slate-800">
                {filteredPlans.map((plan) => {
                  const isActive = plan.is_active ?? plan.status === 'active';
                  const tType = plan.trigger_type || plan.triggerType;
                  const intDays = plan.interval_days || plan.intervalDays;
                  const intMeter = plan.interval_meter || plan.intervalMeter;
                  const assetName = plan.asset_name || plan.assetName || 'Ativo Corporativo';
                  const assetCode = plan.asset_code || plan.assetCode || '';
                  const checklistCount = Array.isArray(plan.checklist)
                    ? plan.checklist.length
                    : Array.isArray(plan.checklist_items)
                    ? plan.checklist_items.length
                    : 0;

                  return (
                    <tr key={plan.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-blue-700 font-mono font-bold block">{plan.code || `PLN-${plan.id.substring(0, 5)}`}</span>
                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-900 font-semibold">{plan.title}</span>
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800 block">{assetName}</span>
                        {assetCode && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono">{assetCode}</span>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        {tType === 'time' && (
                          <span className="text-slate-700 font-medium block">A cada {intDays} dias</span>
                        )}
                        {tType === 'meter' && (
                          <span className="text-slate-700 font-medium block">A cada {intMeter?.toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} km/hrs</span>
                        )}
                        {tType === 'both' && (
                          <span className="text-slate-700 font-medium block">
                            {intDays} dias ou {intMeter?.toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} km
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold">
                          <CheckSquare size={12} />
                          <span>{checklistCount} itens</span>
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        {isActive ? (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold">
                            <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
                            <span>Ativo</span>
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border border-slate-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold">
                            <Pause size={12} />
                            <span>Pausado</span>
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleGenerateWO(plan)}
                            className="bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200 px-2 py-1 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                            title="Gerar Ordem de Serviço Preventiva Imediata"
                          >
                            <Zap size={12} />
                            <span>Gerar OS</span>
                          </button>

                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleToggleActive(plan)}
                            className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                            title={isActive ? 'Pausar Plano' : 'Ativar Plano'}
                          >
                            {isActive ? <Pause size={14} /> : <Play size={14} />}
                          </button>

                          {!isTechnician && (
                            <>
                              <button
                                onClick={() => handleOpenEditModal(plan)}
                                className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                title="Editar Plano"
                              >
                                <Edit2 size={14} />
                              </button>

                              <button
                                onClick={() => handleDeletePlan(plan.id)}
                                className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                title="Eliminar Plano"
                              >
                                <Trash2 size={14} />
                              </button>
                            </>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      )}

      {/* CAMADA SECUNDÁRIA: CRIAÇÃO / EDIÇÃO DE PLANO PREVENTIVO */}
      <SecondaryView
        isOpen={isModalOpen}
        onClose={handleRequestCloseModal}
        title={editingPlan ? 'Editar Plano Preventivo' : 'Novo Plano de Manutenção Preventiva'}
        subtitle="Configuração de periodicidade, checklists e atribuição de técnicos"
        icon={<CalendarClock className="text-blue-700" size={20} />}
        maxWidth="max-w-3xl"
      >
        <div className="p-6">
          {hasRestoredDraft && (
            <div className="mb-4">
              <DraftBanner
                onDiscard={handleDiscardRestoredDraft}
